15: /**
16:  * api/lib/UniversalSearch.php — one search for everything, answered in place (decisions/0053).
17:  *
18:  * understand(): the query becomes chips the person can see and remove.
19:  *   place ("Greer Arizona", "in Round Valley"): a place name wins over a business name unless the whole query is
20:  *     exactly a business's name; category ("physical therapy"); kind ("groups", "stories"); conditions ("has phone",
21:  *     "has website"); facts ("population", "demographics", "median age", "income"). What's left is the search text.
22:  * search(): grouped results, each routed to its home: places, a facts answer, listings (peek first), stories and
23:  *   guides, community groups, topics, and a hand-off to the Address Book for people (people are never in this search).
24:  * Nothing is stored about the query here (decisions/0053 §7).
25:  */
26: require_once __DIR__ . '/CategoryAlso.php';

68:     private const DESCRIBE = ['new', 'newest', 'latest', 'recent', 'good', 'best', 'great', 'nice', 'local', 'little', 'favorite', 'popular', 'cheap', 'a', 'an', 'the', 'some', 'any',

70:     /**
71:      * "Near me" and its kin (decisions/0067, 2026-10-08): where to search, never words to match. Taken out before anything
72:      * reads the words, so "me" is never Maine or the start of "medical", and "concerts near me" searches concerts.
73:      * Longest first.
74:      */
75:     private const NEAR_ME = 'close to me|close by me|near to me|closest to me|nearest to me|near me|nearme|around me|by me|nearby|near by|close by|closest|nearest|near here|around here';
76:     /** Other words that mean where the person is (decisions/0067, 2026-10-08): kept as words, but they need a place. */
77:     private const HERE_WORDS = '/\\b(local|locally|in my area|my area|in town|around town|walking distance|driving distance|within \\d+ ?(mi|mile|miles|km)|\\d+ ?(mi|miles) (away|from)|directions to|how far|how close)\\b/u';

79:     /** Whether the words need to know where the person is to mean anything: "near me" and its kin, "local", "in town", "how far". */
80:     public static function saysHere(string $q): bool
81:     {
82:         return self::nearMe($q)['near'] || (bool) preg_match(self::HERE_WORDS, mb_strtolower($q));
83:     }
84: 
85:     /** The phrases offered when one is still being typed ("near m" → near me), in order. */
86:     private const NEAR_ME_SAY = ['near me', 'nearby', 'nearest', 'closest', 'close to me', 'close by', 'around me'];
87: 
88:     /**
89:      * The words without "near me" (and its kin), whether it was said, and the start of one still being typed at the end
90:      * ("concerts near m": "near m"). ['text' => 'concerts', 'near' => bool, 'partial' => 'near m'|''].
91:      */
92:     public static function nearMe(string $q): array
93:     {
94:         $t = ' ' . trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', mb_strtolower($q))) . ' ';
95:         $near = false; $partial = '';
96:         if (preg_match('/ (?:' . self::NEAR_ME . ') /u', $t)) {
97:             $near = true;
98:             do { $t0 = $t; $t = (string) preg_replace('/ (?:' . self::NEAR_ME . ') /u', ' ', $t); } while ($t !== $t0);
99:         } elseif (preg_match('/ (near|near m|near t|near to|near to m|nearb|neare|neares|close t|close to|close to m|close b|closes|around m)$/u', rtrim($t), $m)) {
100:             // the start of one, still being typed; "near" alone may yet be "near show low", so it's only offered, never assumed
101:             $partial = $m[1];
102:             $t = mb_substr(rtrim($t), 0, -mb_strlen($m[0])) . ' ';
103:         }
104:         return ['text' => trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', $t)), 'near' => $near, 'partial' => $partial];
105:     }
106: 
107:     /**
108:      * Outings (Jason, 2026-11-10: "Kids? Ages? Type of activities? We have an Intelligence built into the search but it
109:      * seems to fall short when sizing up the context"): who's coming (kids, toddlers, teens, the family, the dog, a date)
110:      * and the kind of day (rainy, indoors, outdoors), said around words that only frame the ask ("place to take my",
111:      * "things to do with"). [phrase => [for, age]], longest first.
112:      */
113:     private const OUTING_WHO = ['little ones' => ['family', 'little'], 'little kids' => ['family', 'little'], 'young kids' => ['family', 'little'],
114:         'small kids' => ['family', 'little'], 'big kids' => ['family', 'kids'], 'older kids' => ['family', 'teens'], 'date night' => ['date', ''],
115:         'grandchildren' => ['family', 'kids'], 'grandkids' => ['family', 'kids'], 'toddlers' => ['family', 'little'], 'toddler' => ['family', 'little'],
116:         'preschoolers' => ['family', 'little'], 'babies' => ['family', 'little'], 'baby' => ['family', 'little'], 'teenagers' => ['family', 'teens'],
117:         'teenager' => ['family', 'teens'], 'tweens' => ['family', 'teens'], 'teens' => ['family', 'teens'], 'teen' => ['family', 'teens'],
118:         'children' => ['family', 'kids'], 'child' => ['family', 'kids'], 'kiddos' => ['family', 'kids'], 'kids' => ['family', 'kids'], 'kid' => ['family', 'kids'],
119:         'families' => ['family', ''], 'family' => ['family', ''], 'puppy' => ['dog', ''], 'pups' => ['dog', ''], 'pup' => ['dog', ''], 'dogs' => ['dog', ''],
120:         'dog' => ['dog', ''], 'girlfriend' => ['date', ''], 'boyfriend' => ['date', ''], 'wife' => ['date', ''], 'husband' => ['date', ''],
121:         'romantic' => ['date', ''], 'date' => ['date', '']];
122:     private const OUTING_SET = ['rainy day' => 'indoor', 'rainy days' => 'indoor', 'too hot' => 'indoor', 'hot day' => 'indoor', 'air conditioned' => 'indoor',
123:         'raining' => 'indoor', 'rainy' => 'indoor', 'rain' => 'indoor', 'indoors' => 'indoor', 'indoor' => 'indoor', 'inside' => 'indoor',
124:         'nice day' => 'outdoor', 'sunny' => 'outdoor', 'outdoors' => 'outdoor', 'outdoor' => 'outdoor', 'outside' => 'outdoor'];
125:     /** Words that only frame an outing ("a place to take my", "fun things to do with the"). */
126:     private const OUTING_FRAME = ['place', 'places', 'somewhere', 'spot', 'spots', 'thing', 'things', 'stuff', 'something', 'anything', 'activity', 'activities',
127:         'fun', 'to', 'do', 'take', 'taking', 'bring', 'bringing', 'with', 'for', 'my', 'our', 'your', 'the', 'a', 'an', 'go', 'going', 'where', 'can', 'could',
128:         'should', 'would', 'i', 'we', 'us', 'me', 'what', 'some', 'good', 'best', 'great', 'cool', 'entertain', 'entertaining', 'keep', 'busy', 'occupy', 'occupied',
129:         'outing', 'outings', 'day', 'days', 'out', 'ideas', 'idea', 'night', 'friendly', 'visit', 'see', 'play', 'let', 'lets', 'and', 'or', 'of', 'on', 'at',
130:         'enjoy', 'around', 'here', 'when', 'its', 'it', 'is', 'are', 'there', 'any', 'free', 'cheap', 'family-friendly', 'kid-friendly', 'dog-friendly', 'pet-friendly',
131:         'things-to-do', 'to-do', 'have', 'has', 'need', 'want', 'like', 'love', 'that', 'which', 'who', 'all', 'year', 'old', 'olds', 'ages', 'age', 'aged'];
132:     /** Time words an outing leaves for the When step ("with kids this weekend"). */
133:     private const OUTING_PASS = ['today', 'tonight', 'tomorrow', 'weekend', 'weekends', 'this', 'now', 'saturday', 'sunday', 'friday', 'afternoon', 'morning', 'evening'];
134:     /** A "friendly" ask beside a thing ("family friendly restaurants"): its words put those first, never hide the rest. */
135:     private const OUTING_BOOST = ['family' => ['family', 'kids', 'children', 'playground', 'kid'], 'dog' => ['dog', 'dogs', 'pet', 'pets', 'patio'],
136:         'date' => ['wine', 'bistro', 'steakhouse', 'fine dining', 'romantic', 'cocktail', 'dessert', 'patio', 'view']];
137: 
138:     /**
139:      * An outing in the words: ['for' => family|dog|date|'', 'age' => little|kids|teens|'', 'setting' => indoor|outdoor|'',
140:      * 'text' => the words left, 'soft' => bool (who it's for beside a thing: orders only), 'said' => [phrases]], or null.
141:      * "kids dentist", "dog grooming", "indoor pool" are things, not outings: null.
142:      */
143:     public static function outing(string $text): ?array
144:     {
145:         $t = ' ' . trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', mb_strtolower(str_replace(['’', "'"], ['', ''], $text)))) . ' ';
146:         $t = (string) preg_replace('/ (\d{1,2})(?: ?(?:-|to) ?\d{1,2})? ?(?:years?|yrs?|y\/?o)( olds?)? /u', ' $1yo ', $t);   // "5 year old", "3-5 yo"
147:         $for = ''; $age = ''; $set = ''; $said = [];
148:         if (preg_match('/ (\d{1,2})yo /u', $t, $am)) { $n = (int) $am[1]; $for = 'family'; $age = $n <= 4 ? 'little' : ($n <= 12 ? 'kids' : 'teens'); $said[] = trim($am[0]); $t = str_replace($am[0], ' ', $t); }
149:         foreach (self::OUTING_WHO as $ph => [$f, $a]) {
150:             if (!preg_match('/ ' . preg_quote($ph, '/') . ' /u', $t)) continue;
151:             if ($for === '') $for = $f;
152:             if ($a !== '' && ($age === '' || $f === $for)) $age = $age ?: $a;
153:             $t = (string) preg_replace('/ ' . preg_quote($ph, '/') . ' /u', ' ', $t); $said[] = $ph;
154:         }
155:         foreach (self::OUTING_SET as $ph => $s) {
156:             if (!preg_match('/ ' . preg_quote($ph, '/') . ' /u', $t)) continue;
157:             if ($set === '') $set = $s;
158:             $t = (string) preg_replace('/ ' . preg_quote($ph, '/') . ' /u', ' ', $t); $said[] = $ph;
159:         }
160:         if (!$said) return null;
161:         $words = array_values(array_filter(explode(' ', trim($t)), fn ($w) => $w !== ''));
162:         $rest = array_values(array_filter($words, fn ($w) => !in_array($w, self::OUTING_FRAME, true) && !in_array($w, self::OUTING_PASS, true)));
163:         $pass = array_values(array_filter($words, fn ($w) => in_array($w, self::OUTING_PASS, true)));
164:         if (!$rest) return ['for' => $for, 'age' => $for === 'family' ? $age : '', 'setting' => $set, 'text' => implode(' ', $pass), 'soft' => false, 'said' => $said];
165:         // a thing with who it's for ("family friendly restaurants", "romantic dinner", "kid friendly pizza"): the thing is
166:         // searched; who it's for puts the fitting ones first
167:         $friendly = (bool) preg_match('/ (friendly|with|for) /u', ' ' . mb_strtolower($text) . ' ') || preg_match('/-friendly/u', mb_strtolower($text));
168:         if ($for !== '' && ($friendly || $for === 'date') && !$set) {
169:             $keep = array_values(array_filter($words, fn ($w) => !in_array($w, ['friendly', 'family-friendly', 'kid-friendly', 'dog-friendly', 'pet-friendly', 'with', 'for', 'my', 'our', 'the', 'a'], true)));
170:             return ['for' => $for, 'age' => '', 'setting' => '', 'text' => implode(' ', $keep), 'soft' => true, 'said' => $said];
171:         }
172:         return null;
173:     }
174: 
175:     /**
176:      * An outing being typed ("place to take my k"), as full words with what they find: the words as they are when they
177:      * already read as an outing, else the last word finished as one word of who's coming or the kind of day (kids,
178:      * toddlers, rainy), the commoner ways first, one per reading. [['label', 'means', 'q', 'kind' => 'outing']], at most two.
179:      */
180:     private static function outingIntents(string $q, bool $end = false): array
181:     {
182:         $q = trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', mb_strtolower($q)));
183:         if (!str_contains($q, ' ')) return [];   // one word ("kids", "dog") is a thing, read as typed
184:         $cands = [];
185:         if (($og = self::outing($q)) && !$og['soft']) $cands[] = $q;
186:         elseif (!$end && preg_match('/^(.+\s)(\S+)$/u', $q, $m) && !in_array($m[2], array_merge(self::OUTING_FRAME, self::OUTING_PASS), true)) {   // "things to do" isn't "things to dogs"
187:             $ph = array_values(array_filter(array_merge(array_keys(self::OUTING_WHO), array_keys(self::OUTING_SET)), fn ($p) => !str_contains($p, ' ') && str_starts_with($p, $m[2]) && $p !== $m[2]));
188:             usort($ph, fn ($a, $b) => [!str_ends_with($a, 's'), mb_strlen($a)] <=> [!str_ends_with($b, 's'), mb_strlen($b)]);   // "kids" before "kiddos" and "kid"
189:             foreach ($ph as $p) $cands[] = $m[1] . $p;
190:         }
191:         $out = []; $seen = [];
192:         foreach ($cands as $c) {
193:             $og = self::outing($c);
194:             if (!$og || $og['soft'] || isset($seen[$l = self::outingLabel($og)])) continue;
195:             $seen[$l] = 1;
196:             $out[] = ['label' => ucfirst($c), 'means' => $l . ': places that suit them, nearest first', 'q' => $c, 'kind' => 'outing'];
197:             if (count($out) >= 2) break;
198:         }
199:         return $out;
200:     }
201: 
202:     /** The outing's chip label: "With toddlers", "With the family · indoors", "Rainy day: indoors", "Date night". */
203:     private static function outingLabel(array $o): string
204:     {
205:         $l = ['little' => 'With little ones', 'kids' => 'With kids', 'teens' => 'With teens'][$o['age']] ?? (['family' => 'With the family', 'dog' => 'With your dog', 'date' => 'Date night'][$o['for']] ?? '');
206:         $s = ['indoor' => 'indoors', 'outdoor' => 'outdoors'][$o['setting']] ?? '';
207:         return $l !== '' ? $l . ($s !== '' ? ' · ' . $s : '') : ucfirst($s);
208:     }

211:     /** The site's sections, named in a query ("in the directory", "community"): results then come from that section only. */

213:     private const FUNNEL_WORDS = ["let's explore" => 'explore', 'lets explore' => 'explore', 'discovery' => 'explore', 'discover' => 'explore',

231:     /**
232:      * @param array $ctx  scope: ['level' => 'cluster'|'geo_hub'|'hub', 'slug' => ...] the page's or visitor's place (may be empty)
233:      * @param array $drop chip types the person removed: 'place', 'scope', 'category', 'kind', 'cond', 'facts'
234:      */
235:     /** Words that name a kind of feature, and are also a town's name somewhere: only a place on their own or after "in". */
236:     private const FEATURE_WORDS = ['lake', 'lakes', 'river', 'creek', 'park', 'springs', 'spring', 'canyon', 'mesa', 'valley', 'beach', 'forest', 'mountain',
237:         'mountains', 'falls', 'rock', 'rocks', 'peak', 'butte', 'ridge', 'pass', 'point', 'island', 'bay', 'harbor', 'hills', 'meadow', 'meadows', 'pines',
238:         'woods', 'trail', 'dam', 'reservoir', 'pond', 'wells', 'center', 'junction', 'city', 'village', 'crossing', 'ranch', 'gap', 'bluff', 'grove'];

240:     /** A step in how the words were read, for the term analysis (api/lib/SearchTrace.php); nothing unless it's on. */
241:     private static function tr(string $w, string $what, string $because = '', $words = []): void
242:     {
243:         if (class_exists('SearchTrace', false) && SearchTrace::active()) SearchTrace::add($w, $what, $because, is_array($words) ? $words : preg_split('/\s+/u', trim((string) $words)));
244:     }
245: 
246:     public static function understand(PDO $pdo, string $q, array $ctx = [], array $drop = []): array
247:     {
248:         $text = ' ' . mb_strtolower(trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $q))) . ' ';
249:         self::tr('How', 'Read the words, in a fixed order', 'Why (actions), conditions, facts, which section, When, Who, help, Why (phrases), kind, Where, What (category). Each step takes its words out before the next looks.');
250:         // one thing spelled two ways: "star gazing", "star gaz" (still typing) are stargazing

490:     /** A named place with a point, for "near <it>": an outdoor place first, then a business; or null. */
491:     private static function landmarkNamed(PDO $pdo, string $name): ?array
492:     {
493:         if (!class_exists('SearchIndex') || !SearchIndex::ready($pdo)) return null;
494:         $name = trim(preg_replace('/^the /', '', $name));
495:         if (!str_contains($name, ' ') && isset(self::whatVocab($pdo)[self::root($name)])) return null;   // "near the park" is no park's name
496:         try {
497:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT label, url, lat, lon FROM search_index WHERE kind IN ('outdoors', 'listing') AND lat IS NOT NULL AND private_location = 0
498:                                  AND label" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE ? ORDER BY kind = 'outdoors' DESC, LENGTH(label) LIMIT 1");
499:             $st->execute([SearchText::esc($name) . '%']);
500:             $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
501:         } catch (Throwable) { return null; }
502:         return $r ? ['label' => (string) $r['label'], 'sub' => 'Near', 'key' => '', 'url' => (string) $r['url'], 'landmark' => true,
503:                      'lat' => (float) $r['lat'], 'lon' => (float) $r['lon']] : null;
504:     }
505: 
506:     /**
507:      * The words the directory knows as things (the What, decisions/0061's 5 W's parser): every word of every category
508:      * name, the everyday words for them, kinds of place and things to do. Roots: "plumbers" is "plumber".
509:      */
510:     private static function whatVocab(PDO $pdo): array
511:     {
512:         static $v = null;
513:         if ($v !== null) return $v;
514:         $v = [];
515:         $add = function (string $s) use (&$v) { foreach (preg_split('/[^\p{L}0-9]+/u', mb_strtolower($s)) ?: [] as $w) if (mb_strlen($w) >= 3) $v[self::root($w)] = 1; };
516:         try { foreach ($pdo->query('SELECT name FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1')->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN) as $n) $add(SearchText::catLabel((string) $n)); } catch (Throwable) {}
517:         foreach (SearchText::synonyms() as $k => $alts) { $add((string) $k); foreach ((array) $alts as $a) $add((string) $a); }
518:         foreach (array_keys(self::TAGS) as $t) $add($t);
519:         foreach (self::ACTIVITIES as $forms) foreach ($forms as $f) $add($f);
520:         foreach (self::ACT_WORDS as $ws) $add($ws);
521:         foreach (self::FEATURE_WORDS as $w) $add($w);
522:         foreach (array_keys(self::KINDS) as $w) $add($w);
523:         foreach (['and', 'the', 'for', 'other', 'retail', 'wholesale', 'new', 'used', 'whls', 'mfrs', 'except', 'misc', 'general'] as $w) unset($v[self::root($w)]);
524:         return $v;
525:     }
526: 
527:     private static function root(string $w): string
528:     {
529:         $w = mb_strtolower($w);
530:         if (mb_strlen($w) > 4 && str_ends_with($w, 'ies')) return mb_substr($w, 0, -3) . 'y';
531:         if (mb_strlen($w) > 4 && preg_match('/(x|ch|sh|ss)es$/', $w)) return mb_substr($w, 0, -2);   // taxes, churches, bushes, classes
532:         if (mb_strlen($w) > 3 && preg_match('/[^s]s$/', $w)) return mb_substr($w, 0, -1);
533:         return $w;
534:     }
535: 
536:     /**
537:      * A category's close kin and whether its word is shared (decisions/0061): "Parks" takes in State Parks and National
538:      * Parks/Preserves; but "park" is also in RV Parks, Mobile Home Parks and Parking, so a business named "…Park" counts
539:      * only if it's in Parks or its kin. "Pizza" is in no other category, so Dittys Pizza & Pie (a restaurant) still counts.
540:      * ['kin' => [ids], 'shared' => bool]
541:      */
542:     private static function catKin(PDO $pdo, array $cat): array
543:     {
544:         $label = SearchText::catLabel((string) $cat['label']);
545:         // the categories that share its plain name are one kind to a visitor ("Self Storage" is three of ours) (decisions/0068)
546:         $same = [];
547:         foreach (SearchText::plainNames($pdo) as $rn => $pn) if (mb_strtolower($pn) === mb_strtolower($label) && $rn !== mb_strtolower((string) $cat['label'])) $same[] = $rn;
548:         $kin = []; $shared = false;
549:         if ($same) try {
550:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND LOWER(name) IN (' . implode(',', array_fill(0, count($same), '?')) . ')');
551:             $st->execute($same);
552:             foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN) as $id) $kin[] = (int) $id;
553:         } catch (Throwable) {}
554:         $w = self::root(mb_strtolower((string) (preg_split('/[^\p{L}]+/u', $label, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY)[0] ?? '')));
555:         if (mb_strlen($w) < 3) return ['kin' => $kin, 'shared' => false];
556:         try {
557:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND id <> ? AND (parent_id IS NULL OR parent_id <> ?) AND LOWER(name) LIKE ?");
558:             $st->execute([(int) $cat['id'], (int) $cat['id'], '%' . $w . '%']);
559:             $q = preg_quote(mb_strtolower($label), '/');
560:             foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) {
561:                 $n = mb_strtolower((string) $c['name']);
562:                 if (in_array((int) $c['id'], $kin, true)) continue;
563:                 if (preg_match('/^(state|national|county|city|public|regional|community|tribal|local) ' . $q . '\b/u', $n)) $kin[] = (int) $c['id'];
564:                 else $shared = true;
565:             }
566:         } catch (Throwable) {}
567:         return ['kin' => array_values(array_unique($kin)), 'shared' => $shared];
568:     }
569: 
570:     /** A category's name has these words together ("auto parts", "oil change"). */
571:     private static function catHas(PDO $pdo, string $phrase): bool
572:     {
573:         try {
574:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT 1 FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND name' . SearchText::CI . " LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' LIMIT 1");
575:             $st->execute(['%' . SearchText::esc($phrase) . '%']);
576:             return (bool) $st->fetchColumn();
577:         } catch (Throwable) { return false; }
578:     }
579: 
580:     /** Something here is named with these words, in this order ("st johns market"): a Who, searched by its name. */
581:     private static function nameHas(PDO $pdo, string $text): bool
582:     {
583:         try {
584:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM search_index WHERE kind IN ('listing', 'outdoors', 'story', 'group', 'experience')
585:                                  AND CONCAT(' ', REPLACE(REPLACE(label, '-', ' '), '&', ' '), ' ')" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE ? LIMIT 1");
586:             $st->execute(['% ' . SearchText::esc($text) . ' %']);
587:             return (bool) $st->fetchColumn();
588:         } catch (Throwable) { return false; }
589:     }
590: 
591:     /** A listing whose name is exactly the query, as a result row; or null. */
592:     private static function exactBusiness(PDO $pdo, string $text): ?array
593:     {
594:         $in = "'" . implode("','", self::STATUSES) . "'";
595:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT e.id, e.name, c.name AS category FROM entities e LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = e.category_id
596:                               WHERE e.entity_type = 'merchant' AND e.is_suppressed = 0 AND e.status IN ($in) AND e.name" . SearchText::CI . " = ? LIMIT 1");
597:         $st->execute([$text]);
598:         $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
599:         return $r ? ['id' => (int) $r['id'], 'label' => (string) $r['name'], 'sub' => (string) ($r['category'] ?? 'Listing'), 'url' => '/listing/view.php?id=' . (int) $r['id'], 'peek' => true] : null;
600:     }

602:     /** [place|null, remaining text]. Tries runs of 1–4 words at the end, then the start; a state name or code narrows it. */
603:     /** Words taught in the Workbench for one kind (decisions/0063): word => class. */
604:     private static function taught(PDO $pdo, string $kind): array
605:     {
606:         static $t = null;
607:         if ($t === null) {
608:             $t = [];
609:             try { foreach ($pdo->query("SELECT kind, class, word FROM search_meanings WHERE kind IN ('audience', 'notplace')")->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $t[$r['kind']][mb_strtolower((string) $r['word'])] = (string) $r['class']; } catch (Throwable) {}
610:         }
611:         return $t[$kind] ?? [];
612:     }
613: 
614:     /** Who it's for: the built-in words, and the ones taught (each to one of the audiences search knows). */
615:     private static function forWhom(PDO $pdo): array
616:     {
617:         $fw = self::FOR_WHOM;
618:         foreach (self::taught($pdo, 'audience') as $w => $a) if (isset(SearchIndex::AUDIENCE_LABEL[$a])) $fw[$w] = $a;
619:         return $fw;
620:     }
621: 
622:     private static function findPlace(PDO $pdo, string $text): array
623:     {
624:         // "washington state": the state, not a town or a listing with "state" in its name
625:         $stRe = '/^(.*?)\s*\b(' . implode('|', array_map(fn ($x) => preg_quote($x, '/'), array_keys(self::STATES))) . ')\s+state$/u';
626:         if (preg_match($stRe, trim($text), $sm)) {
627:             if (trim($sm[1], ' ,') === '' && ($sp = self::stateNamed($pdo, self::STATES[$sm[2]]))) return [$sp, ''];
628:             $text = trim($sm[1]) . ' ' . $sm[2];
629:         }
630:         $w = explode(' ', $text);
631:         $state = null;
632:         // a trailing state ("greer arizona", "show low, az")
633:         for ($k = min(2, count($w) - 1); $k >= 1; $k--) {
634:             $tail = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($w, -$k)), ' ,');
635:             $abbr = self::STATES[$tail] ?? (strlen($tail) === 2 && in_array(strtoupper($tail), self::STATES, true) ? strtoupper($tail) : null);
636:             if ($abbr && count($w) > $k) { $state = $abbr; $w = array_slice($w, 0, -$k); break; }
637:         }
638:         // a state's full name first, with the thing after it ("arizona food", "new mexico hiking": Jason, 2026-11-11, the
639:         // words as typed are the query); never a two-letter code here ("in", "or", "me" are words)
640:         if (!$state) for ($k = min(2, count($w) - 1); $k >= 1; $k--) {
641:             $head = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($w, 0, $k)), ' ,');
642:             if (isset(self::STATES[$head]) && count($w) > $k) { $state = self::STATES[$head]; $w = array_slice($w, $k); break; }
643:         }
644:         $n = count($w);
645:         // the whole search is the exact name of something we have ("laguna art museum", "fool hollow lake"): that's what they
646:         // mean, so a town or local name in some of its words isn't read (Laguna, NM; Fool Hollow). As in cityFirst; the
647:         // words as a whole may still be a place ("show low").
648:         $whole = trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', str_replace(',', ' ', $text)));
649:         $named = substr_count($whole, ' ') >= 1 && self::namedExactly($pdo, $whole);
650:         for ($len = min(4, $n); $len >= 1; $len--) {
651:             if ($named && $len < $n) break;
652:             foreach ([[$n - $len, 'end'], [0, 'start']] as [$from, $side]) {
653:                 $phrase = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($w, $from, $len)), ' ,');
654:                 if (mb_strlen($phrase) < 3) continue;
655:                 // "st johns" is Saint Johns, "mt" Mount, "ft" Fort, as the ZIP list spells them
656:                 $phrase = preg_replace(['/^st\.? /', '/^mt\.? /', '/^ft\.? /'], ['saint ', 'mount ', 'fort '], $phrase);
657:                 if (isset(self::taught($pdo, 'notplace')[mb_strtolower($phrase)])) continue;   // taught as never a place (decisions/0063)
658:                 // a lone feature word that is also a town somewhere ("Lake", MI; "Park", KS; "Springs") is part of a
659:                 // name when other words come with it: "Lyman lake" is a lake, not Lake, Michigan. It's a place only
660:                 // after "in", "near", "at" or "around".
661:                 if ($len === 1 && $n > 1 && in_array(mb_strtolower($phrase), self::FEATURE_WORDS, true)) {
662:                     $before = mb_strtolower((string) ($w[$from - 1] ?? ''));
663:                     if (!in_array($before, ['in', 'near', 'at', 'around'], true)) continue;
664:                 }
665:                 // a town outside our areas counts only when it's the whole search, comes after "in/near/at/around", or has
666:                 // its state: "four corn" isn't Corn, Oklahoma (decisions/0058 §25)
667:                 $prev = mb_strtolower((string) ($w[$from - 1] ?? ''));
668:                 // ...and a town outside our regions needs "in/near/at/around" or its state, even typed alone: "Lyman" is
669:                 // Lyman Lake by St. Johns, not Lyman, Nebraska (decisions/0060)
670:                 $strict = !in_array($prev, ['in', 'near', 'at', 'around'], true);
671:                 if ($p = self::placeNamed($pdo, $phrase, $state, $strict)) {
672:                     $rest = $side === 'end' ? array_slice($w, 0, $n - $len) : array_slice($w, $len);
673:                     return [$p, trim(implode(' ', $rest), ' ,')];
674:                 }
675:             }
676:         }
677:         // a state and no town in it named ("pizza in washington", "arizona"): the state is the place
678:         if ($state && ($sp = self::stateNamed($pdo, $state))) return [$sp, trim(implode(' ', $w), ' ,')];
679:         return [null, trim(implode(' ', $w) . '', ' ,')];
680:     }
681: 
682:     /**
683:      * The city in the words, as typed (CLAUDE.md, Places in search): the longest run of words at the end, at the start, or
684:      * after "in / near / at / around / to", with or without its state, that names a city in the locations data or one of
685:      * our places. A city counts whether or not we have anything there. Guards kept: a lone feature word ("lake", "point")
686:      * among other words, or a lone word that's a kind of thing ("golf", "price"), is a city only with its state or after
687:      * "in"; words taught as never a place stay so. Returns [place or null, the other words].
688:      */
689:     private static function cityFirst(PDO $pdo, string $text): array
690:     {
691:         // the whole search is the exact name of something we have ("four corners monument", "laguna art museum"): that's
692:         // what they mean, not a town in its first words (Jason, 2026-11-10). Two words or more, so "phoenix" stays the city.
693:         $whole = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', str_replace(',', ' ', $text)));
694:         if (substr_count($whole, ' ') >= 1 && self::namedExactly($pdo, $whole)) return [null, $text];
695:         $w = array_values(array_filter(explode(' ', str_replace(',', ' , ', $text)), fn ($x) => $x !== ''));
696:         $state = null; $stateAt = null;
697:         $vocab = self::whatVocab($pdo); $not = self::taught($pdo, 'notplace');
698:         $cue = ['in', 'near', 'at', 'around', 'to'];
699:         $tryRun = function (int $from, int $len) use (&$w, $pdo, $vocab, $not, $cue, &$state): ?array {
700:             $words = array_slice($w, $from, $len);
701:             if (in_array(',', $words, true)) return null;
702:             $phrase = trim(implode(' ', $words));
703:             if (mb_strlen($phrase) < 3 || preg_match('/\d/', $phrase)) return null;
704:             $phrase = preg_replace(['/^st\.? /', '/^mt\.? /', '/^ft\.? /'], ['saint ', 'mount ', 'fort '], $phrase);
705:             if (isset($not[$phrase])) return null;
706:             $cued = in_array((string) ($w[$from - 1] ?? ''), $cue, true);
707:             $alone = count(array_filter($w, fn ($x) => $x !== ',')) === $len;
708:             if ($len === 1 && !$state && !$cued) {
709:                 if (!$alone && in_array($phrase, self::FEATURE_WORDS, true)) return null;   // "lyman lake" is a lake
710:                 if (isset($vocab[self::root($phrase)])) return null;   // "golf", "price": a thing, unless "in golf" or "golf, il"
711:             }
712:             if ($len === 1 && in_array($phrase, array_merge(self::FILLER, self::DESCRIBE), true)) return null;
713:             $p = self::placeNamed($pdo, $phrase, $state, false, true);
714:             if (!$p || !empty($p['is_state'])) return null;
715:             // a name used in several states, none of them ours ("springfield"): search asks Which Springfield? (whichPlace)
716:             if (!$state && !empty($p['outside'])) {
717:                 require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
718:                 $ns = $pdo->prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT state) FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ?'); $ns->execute([(string) ($p['city'] ?? $phrase)]);
719:                 if ((int) $ns->fetchColumn() > 1 && !in_array((string) ($p['state'] ?? ''), NpiBulk::STATES, true)) return null;
720:             }
721:             // one word beside others, no "in" and no state ("best pizza"): a town only if it's one of ours, in the home
722:             // states, or big enough that people mean it ("pizza phoenix")
723:             if ($len === 1 && !$alone && !$cued && !$state && !empty($p['outside'])) {
724:                 require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
725:                 if (!in_array((string) ($p['state'] ?? ''), NpiBulk::STATES, true)) {
726:                     $pp = $pdo->prepare('SELECT COALESCE(SUM(population), 0) FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ? AND state = ?'); $pp->execute([(string) ($p['city'] ?? $phrase), (string) ($p['state'] ?? '')]);
727:                     if ((int) $pp->fetchColumn() < 20000) return null;
728:                 }
729:             }
730:             return $p;
731:         };
732:         // a trailing state ("phoenix az", "dana point, california")
733:         $n = count($w);
734:         for ($k = min(2, $n - 1); $k >= 1; $k--) {
735:             $tail = trim(implode(' ', array_slice($w, -$k)), ' ,');
736:             $abbr = self::STATES[$tail] ?? (strlen($tail) === 2 && in_array(strtoupper($tail), self::STATES, true) ? strtoupper($tail) : null);
737:             if ($abbr && $n > $k) { $state = $abbr; $w = array_slice($w, 0, -$k); break; }
738:         }
739:         while ($w && end($w) === ',') array_pop($w);
740:         $n = count($w);
741:         $rest = function (int $from, int $len) use (&$w, $cue): string {
742:             $before = array_slice($w, 0, $from); $after = array_slice($w, $from + $len);
743:             if ($before && in_array(end($before), $cue, true)) array_pop($before);   // "in", "near": part of saying where
744:             return trim(preg_replace('/\s*,\s*/', ' ', implode(' ', array_merge($before, $after))));
745:         };
746:         for ($len = min(4, $n); $len >= 1; $len--) {
747:             $starts = [$n - $len, 0];
748:             // after "in" anywhere, unless the name runs on: "near lyman lake" is the lake, not Lyman; "in phoenix tonight" is Phoenix
749:             foreach ($w as $i => $x) if (in_array($x, $cue, true) && $i + 1 + $len <= $n && !in_array((string) ($w[$i + 1 + $len] ?? ''), self::FEATURE_WORDS, true)) $starts[] = $i + 1;
750:             foreach (array_unique($starts) as $from) {
751:                 if ($from < 0) continue;
752:                 if ($p = $tryRun($from, $len)) return [$p, $rest($from, $len)];
753:             }
754:         }
755:         return [null, $text];
756:     }
757: 
758:     /** A landmark, outdoor place or business on Traversence named exactly these words (not just starting with them). */
759:     private static function namedExactly(PDO $pdo, string $phrase): bool
760:     {
761:         static $seen = [];   // asked by cityFirst and findPlace for the same words
762:         $k = mb_strtolower($phrase);
763:         if (!isset($seen[$k])) $seen[$k] = self::namedExactlyAsk($pdo, $phrase);
764:         return $seen[$k];
765:     }
766: 
767:     private static function namedExactlyAsk(PDO $pdo, string $phrase): bool
768:     {
769:         try {
770:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM search_index WHERE kind IN ('outdoors', 'listing') AND label" . SearchText::CI . " = ? LIMIT 1");
771:             $st->execute([$phrase]);
772:             if ($st->fetchColumn()) return true;
773:             require_once __DIR__ . '/Landmarks.php';
774:             if (!Landmarks::ready($pdo)) return false;
775:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT 1 FROM landmarks WHERE name' . SearchText::CI . ' = ? AND ' . Landmarks::visibleSql() . ' LIMIT 1');
776:             $st->execute([$phrase]);
777:             return (bool) $st->fetchColumn();
778:         } catch (Throwable) { return false; }
779:     }

781:     private static function clusterRow(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array
782:     {
783:         $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id, name, slug, primary_zip, associated_zips FROM micro_clusters WHERE id = ?');
784:         $st->execute([$id]);
785:         return $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: null;
786:     }

788:     private static function placeOut(PDO $pdo, string $level, int $id, string $label, string $slug, array $zips, string $sub): array
789:     {
790:         $key = $level . ':' . $id;
791:         try { [, $url] = Links::resolve($pdo, 'place', $key); } catch (InvalidArgumentException) { $url = ''; }
792:         return ['label' => $label, 'sub' => $sub, 'key' => $key, 'level' => $level, 'slug' => $slug, 'url' => $url, 'zips' => $zips];
793:     }
794: 
795:     /** A place by exact name: a town (via its ZIPs to its cluster), a cluster or its accepted local name, an area, a region. */
796:     /** An outdoor place, listing or landmark named these words, or starting with them as whole words ("Four Corners Monument"). */
797:     private static function namesSomething(PDO $pdo, string $phrase): bool
798:     {
799:         try {
800:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM search_index WHERE kind IN ('outdoors', 'listing') AND (label" . SearchText::CI . " = ? OR label" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE ?) LIMIT 1");
801:             $st->execute([$phrase, str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\\%', '\\_'], $phrase) . ' %']);
802:             if ($st->fetchColumn()) return true;
803:             // a landmark kept but not shown (on a nation's land, until the nation tools) still isn't a far-off town
804:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT 1 FROM landmarks WHERE status <> 'hidden' AND (name" . SearchText::CI . " = ? OR name" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE ?) LIMIT 1");
805:             $st->execute([$phrase, str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\\%', '\\_'], $phrase) . ' %']);
806:             return (bool) $st->fetchColumn();
807:         } catch (Throwable) { return false; }
808:     }
809: 
810:     private static function placeNamed(PDO $pdo, string $phrase, ?string $state, bool $strict = false, bool $city = false): ?array
811:     {
812:         $ci = SearchText::CI;
813:         // a cluster by its public name, or an accepted local name
814:         static $clusters = null;   // name => row, loaded once per request
815:         if ($clusters === null) {
816:             $clusters = [];
817:             foreach ($pdo->query('SELECT id, name, slug, primary_zip, associated_zips FROM micro_clusters')->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) {
818:                 $pub = mb_strtolower(PlaceScope::publicName((string) $c['name']));
819:                 $clusters[$pub] ??= $c;
820:                 $clusters[trim(preg_replace('/\s*\(.*\)$/', '', $pub))] ??= $c;
821:             }
822:         }
823:         // a county ("navajo county", "king county, wa"): every ZIP in it, its middle, and the state (decisions/0066)
824:         if (preg_match('/^(.{2,40}?) county$/u', $phrase, $cm) && ($co = self::countyNamed($pdo, trim($cm[1]), $state))) return $co;
825:         if (isset($clusters[$phrase])) {
826:             $c = $clusters[$phrase];
827:             // an area named for the town it's in ("Phoenix", "Glendale"): the town is the place, with all its own ZIPs and its
828:             // state ("Phoenix, AZ"), as any town named is. An area's few ZIPs left "pizza in phoenix" with only OpenStreetMap,
829:             // and "Glendale" read without its state (2026-11-11). Its area stays its own (url, slug).
830:             $elsewhere = false;
831:             if ($tw = self::townOfArea($pdo, $c, $phrase, $state, $elsewhere)) return $tw;
832:             // another state's town of that name, typed with its state ("phoenix, or"): that town, below
833:             if (!$elsewhere) return self::placeOut($pdo, 'cluster', (int) $c['id'], PlaceScope::publicName((string) $c['name']), (string) $c['slug'], self::zipsOf($c), 'Town');
834:         }
835:         try {
836:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT cluster_id, name FROM cluster_subgroups WHERE status = 'accepted' AND name$ci = ? LIMIT 1");
837:             $st->execute([$phrase]);
838:             if ($sg = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) {
839:                 $c = self::clusterRow($pdo, (int) $sg['cluster_id']);
840:                 if ($c) return self::placeOut($pdo, 'cluster', (int) $c['id'], (string) $sg['name'], (string) $c['slug'], self::zipsOf($c), 'Local name');
841:             }
842:         } catch (PDOException) {}
843:         // a town in the ZIP list, then the cluster it belongs to
844:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT zip, TRIM(state) AS state, TRIM(city) AS city, COALESCE(population, 0) AS pop, latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ?" . ($state ? ' AND state = ?' : '') . ' LIMIT 600');
845:         $st->execute($state ? [$phrase, $state] : [$phrase]);
846:         $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
847:         // the same name typed without its punctuation or spacing ("coeur d alene" is Coeur d'Alene, "winston salem")
848:         if (!$rows && ($sq = self::squash($phrase)) !== '' && mb_strlen($sq) >= 4) {
849:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT zip, TRIM(state) AS state, TRIM(city) AS city, COALESCE(population, 0) AS pop FROM zip_coordinates WHERE ' . self::squashSql('city') . ' = ?' . ($state ? ' AND TRIM(state) = ?' : '') . ' LIMIT 600');
850:             $st->execute($state ? [$sq, $state] : [$sq]);
851:             $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
852:         }
853:         // the pilot states' town first ("Four Corners" is also a town in Wyoming and Vermont)
854:         require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
855:         $home = fn (array $r) => in_array($r['state'], NpiBulk::STATES, true) ? 1 : 0;
856:         $people = []; foreach ($rows as $r) $people[$r['state']] = ($people[$r['state']] ?? 0) + (int) $r['pop'];   // then the most people ("springfield": Illinois's)
857:         // the one near the person first, within 150 mi (Glendale, AZ to someone in Phoenix, not Glendale, CA)
858:         $close = []; if (self::$near) foreach ($rows as $r) if (isset($r['latitude']) && $r['latitude'] !== null && self::mi(self::$near[0], self::$near[1], (float) $r['latitude'], (float) $r['longitude']) <= 150) $close[$r['state']] = 1;
859:         usort($rows, fn ($a, $b) => [isset($close[$b['state']]) ? 1 : 0, $home($b), $people[$b['state']]] <=> [isset($close[$a['state']]) ? 1 : 0, $home($a), $people[$a['state']]]);
860:         if ($rows) $rows = array_values(array_filter($rows, fn ($r) => $r['state'] === $rows[0]['state']));   // one state's ZIPs, never Phoenix, AZ with Phoenix, OR
861:         if ($rows) {
862:             $zips = array_values(array_unique(array_map(fn ($r) => trim((string) $r['zip']), $rows)));
863:             $town = ucwords((string) $rows[0]['city']) . ', ' . $rows[0]['state'];
864:             if ($c = self::clusterForZips($pdo, $zips)) {
865:                 $area = PlaceScope::publicName((string) $c['name']);
866:                 return ['label' => $town, 'sub' => preg_match('/^ZIP \d/', $area) || mb_strtolower($area) === mb_strtolower((string) $rows[0]['city']) ? 'Town' : 'Town in ' . $area] + array_diff_key(self::placeOut($pdo, 'cluster', (int) $c['id'], $town, (string) $c['slug'], $zips, ''), ['label' => 1, 'sub' => 1]) + ['town' => true];
867:             }
868:             // a town outside our areas, named without its state, is only the place when nothing on Traversence has that
869:             // name: "Four Corners" is the monument, not Four Corners, Wyoming (decisions/0058 §25)
870:             if (!$city && !$state && ($strict || self::namesSomething($pdo, $phrase))) return null;   // a city read first (cityFirst) is always the place
871:             return ['label' => $town, 'sub' => 'Town', 'key' => '', 'level' => '', 'slug' => '', 'url' => '', 'zips' => $zips, 'town' => true, 'city' => (string) $rows[0]['city'], 'state' => (string) $rows[0]['state'], 'outside' => true];   // outside our areas (decisions/0060, 2026-10-04 notes)
872:         }
873:         foreach (['geo_hub' => 'geo_hubs', 'hub' => 'hubs'] as $level => $table) {
874:             try {
875:                 $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name, slug FROM $table WHERE name$ci = ? LIMIT 1");
876:                 $st->execute([$phrase]);
877:                 if ($r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) return self::placeOut($pdo, $level, (int) $r['id'], (string) $r['name'], (string) $r['slug'], [], $level === 'hub' ? 'Region' : 'Area');
878:             } catch (PDOException) {}
879:         }
880:         return null;
881:     }
882: 
883:     /**
884:      * The town an area of ours is named for, when the area's own main ZIP is in that town ("Phoenix" is Phoenix, AZ): a
885:      * town place in the area, with the town's ZIPs (the area's main ZIP first, so its middle stays where it was, then the
886:      * town's by how many people, then the rest of the area's) and its state. Null when the area's name isn't its town's;
887:      * $elsewhere says a state was typed that isn't the area's ("phoenix, or" is Phoenix, Oregon).
888:      */
889:     private static function townOfArea(PDO $pdo, array $c, string $phrase, ?string $state, bool &$elsewhere = false): ?array
890:     {
891:         try {
892:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(city) AS city, TRIM(state) AS state FROM zip_coordinates WHERE zip = ? LIMIT 1');
893:             $st->execute([trim((string) $c['primary_zip'])]);
894:             $z = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
895:             $elsewhere = $z && $state && $state !== (string) $z['state'];   // the area is in another state than the one typed
896:             if (!$z || $elsewhere || self::squash((string) $z['city']) !== self::squash($phrase)) return null;
897:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(zip) FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ? AND state = ? ORDER BY COALESCE(population, 0) DESC LIMIT 600');
898:             $st->execute([(string) $z['city'], (string) $z['state']]);
899:             $zips = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_merge([trim((string) $c['primary_zip'])], array_map('strval', $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN)), self::zipsOf($c)))));
900:         } catch (Throwable) { return null; }
901:         $town = ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $z['city'])) . ', ' . $z['state'];
902:         return ['label' => $town, 'sub' => 'Town'] + array_diff_key(self::placeOut($pdo, 'cluster', (int) $c['id'], $town, (string) $c['slug'], $zips, ''), ['label' => 1, 'sub' => 1])
903:              + ['city' => (string) $z['city'], 'state' => (string) $z['state']];   // not 'town': the area is the town, so all of it still counts
904:     }
905: 
906:     /**
907:      * A county by name, in the state given or, with none, the pilot states first, then wherever the name is used most:
908:      * ['label' => 'Navajo County, AZ', 'sub' => 'County', 'county', 'state', 'zips', 'lat', 'lon', 'outside'].
909:      */
910:     private static function countyNamed(PDO $pdo, string $name, ?string $state): ?array
911:     {
912:         $ci = SearchText::CI;
913:         $bare = preg_replace('/\s+county$/i', '', trim($name));
914:         try {
915:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT TRIM(state) AS state, COUNT(*) AS n FROM zip_coordinates WHERE (TRIM(county)$ci = ? OR TRIM(county)$ci = ?)" . ($state ? ' AND TRIM(state) = ?' : '') . ' GROUP BY TRIM(state)');
916:             $st->execute($state ? [$bare, $bare . ' County', $state] : [$bare, $bare . ' County']);
917:             $states = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
918:         } catch (Throwable) { return null; }
919:         if (!$states) return null;
920:         require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
921:         usort($states, fn ($a, $b) => [in_array($b['state'], NpiBulk::STATES, true), (int) $b['n']] <=> [in_array($a['state'], NpiBulk::STATES, true), (int) $a['n']]);
922:         $stt = (string) $states[0]['state'];
923:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT TRIM(zip) AS zip, TRIM(county) AS county, latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE (TRIM(county)$ci = ? OR TRIM(county)$ci = ?) AND TRIM(state) = ?");
924:         $st->execute([$bare, $bare . ' County', $stt]);
925:         $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
926:         if (!$rows) return null;
927:         $zips = array_values(array_unique(array_column($rows, 'zip')));
928:         $pts = array_filter($rows, fn ($r) => $r['latitude'] !== null);
929:         $lat = $pts ? array_sum(array_map(fn ($r) => (float) $r['latitude'], $pts)) / count($pts) : null;
930:         $lon = $pts ? array_sum(array_map(fn ($r) => (float) $r['longitude'], $pts)) / count($pts) : null;
931:         $county = (string) $rows[0]['county'];
932:         $label = (preg_match('/county$/i', $county) ? ucwords(mb_strtolower($county)) : ucwords(mb_strtolower($county)) . ' County') . ', ' . $stt;
933:         $p = ['label' => $label, 'sub' => 'County', 'key' => '', 'level' => '', 'slug' => '', 'url' => '', 'zips' => $zips, 'county' => $county, 'state' => $stt,
934:               'outside' => !self::clusterForZips($pdo, array_slice($zips, 0, 200))];
935:         if ($lat !== null) $p += ['lat' => round($lat, 4), 'lon' => round($lon, 4)];
936:         return $p;
937:     }
938: 
939:     /**
940:      * A state as a place: every ZIP in it, its middle, and whether we have any areas there:
941:      * ['label' => 'Washington', 'sub' => 'State', 'state' => 'WA', 'is_state' => true, 'lat', 'lon', 'outside'].
942:      */
943:     private static function stateNamed(PDO $pdo, string $abbr): ?array
944:     {
945:         $name = array_search($abbr, self::STATES, true);
946:         if ($name === false) return null;
947:         try {
948:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT AVG(latitude) AS lat, AVG(longitude) AS lon FROM zip_coordinates WHERE state = ?');
949:             $st->execute([$abbr]);
950:             $c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: [];
951:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT 1 FROM micro_clusters mc JOIN zip_coordinates zc ON TRIM(zc.zip) = mc.primary_zip WHERE TRIM(zc.state) = ? LIMIT 1');
952:             $st->execute([$abbr]);
953:             $ours = (bool) $st->fetchColumn();
954:         } catch (Throwable) { $c = []; $ours = false; }
955:         $p = ['label' => ucwords($name), 'sub' => 'State', 'key' => '', 'level' => '', 'slug' => '', 'url' => '', 'zips' => [], 'state' => $abbr, 'is_state' => true, 'outside' => !$ours];
956:         if (($c['lat'] ?? null) !== null) $p += ['lat' => round((float) $c['lat'], 4), 'lon' => round((float) $c['lon'], 4)];
957:         return $p;
958:     }
959: 
960:     /** A name for comparing whatever its punctuation and spacing: "Coeur d'Alene" and "coeur d alene" are "coeurdalene". */
961:     private static function squash(string $s): string { return (string) preg_replace('/[^a-z0-9]+/', '', mb_strtolower($s)); }
962: 
963:     /** The same, in SQL, for a column. */
964:     private static function squashSql(string $col): string
965:     {
966:         // the town's name squashed, kept in zip_coordinates.city_key with an index (2026-11-22_zip_city_index.sql), so
967:         // a town is found without reading all 41,000 ZIPs
968:         if ($col === 'city' && self::hasCityKey()) return 'city_key';
969:         $x = "LOWER(TRIM($col))";
970:         foreach ([' ', "''", '.', '-', '’', ','] as $c) $x = "REPLACE($x, '$c', '')";   // '' is a quote inside SQL
971:         return $x;
972:     }
973: 
974:     private static ?bool $cityKey = null;
975: 
976:     private static function hasCityKey(): bool
977:     {
978:         if (self::$cityKey === null) { try { Database::connection()->query('SELECT city_key FROM zip_coordinates LIMIT 0'); self::$cityKey = true; } catch (Throwable) { self::$cityKey = false; } }
979:         return self::$cityKey;
980:     }
981: 
982:     private static function zipsOf(array $c): array
983:     {
984:         return array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_merge([(string) $c['primary_zip']], (array) (json_decode((string) ($c['associated_zips'] ?? ''), true) ?: [])))));
985:     }
986: 
987:     private static function clusterForZips(PDO $pdo, array $zips): ?array
988:     {
989:         $zips = array_slice($zips, 0, 10);
990:         $in = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($zips), '?'));
991:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name, slug, primary_zip, associated_zips FROM micro_clusters WHERE primary_zip IN ($in) LIMIT 1");
992:         $st->execute($zips);
993:         if ($c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) return $c;
994:         $or = implode(' OR ', array_fill(0, count($zips), 'associated_zips LIKE ?'));
995:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name, slug, primary_zip, associated_zips FROM micro_clusters WHERE $or LIMIT 1");
996:         $st->execute(array_map(fn ($z) => '%"' . $z . '"%', $zips));
997:         return $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: null;
998:     }
999: 
1000:     /** The visitor's current place (a scope) as a place chip, or null. */
1001:     public static function scopePlace(PDO $pdo, array $ctx): ?array { return self::hasScope($ctx) ? self::placeFromScope($pdo, $ctx) : null; }
1002: 
1003:     /** A place is set on the site: one of our areas (a slug), or any town, ZIP or point someone chose ("Seattle, WA"). */
1004:     private static function hasScope(array $ctx): bool
1005:     {
1006:         return !empty($ctx['slug']) || !empty($ctx['city']) || !empty($ctx['zip']) || (isset($ctx['lat'], $ctx['lon']) && $ctx['lat'] !== null);
1007:     }
1008: 
1009:     /**
1010:      * A place set by state, city or ZIP, or found by Auto-detect, with no area of ours named. When its ZIPs (or, for a
1011:      * bare point, the nearest town's) are in one of our areas, it's that area's town, like a town chosen there. Outside
1012:      * our areas ("Seattle, WA") it's still the person's place. Its ZIPs and middle come from the ZIP list when we have
1013:      * them, else from the point they gave. Search answers there with what's near it, or says nothing is listed there
1014:      * yet; it never asks where again.
1015:      */
1016:     private static function loosePlace(PDO $pdo, array $ctx): ?array
1017:     {
1018:         $city = trim((string) ($ctx['city'] ?? '')); $state = strtoupper(trim((string) ($ctx['state'] ?? '')));
1019:         if (!preg_match('/^[A-Z]{2}$/', $state)) $state = '';
1020:         $zip = preg_match('/^\d{5}$/', trim((string) ($ctx['zip'] ?? ''))) ? trim((string) $ctx['zip']) : '';
1021:         $lat = isset($ctx['lat']) && is_numeric($ctx['lat']) ? (float) $ctx['lat'] : null;
1022:         $lon = isset($ctx['lon']) && is_numeric($ctx['lon']) ? (float) $ctx['lon'] : null;
1023:         $zips = [];
1024:         try {
1025:             if ($zip !== '') {
1026:                 $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(city) AS city, TRIM(state) AS state, latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE zip = ? LIMIT 1');
1027:                 $st->execute([$zip]);
1028:                 if ($z = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) { $city = $city ?: ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $z['city'])); $state = $state ?: (string) $z['state']; $lat ??= (float) $z['latitude']; $lon ??= (float) $z['longitude']; }
1029:                 $zips = [$zip];
1030:             } elseif ($city !== '') {
1031:                 $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT zip, latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ?' . ($state !== '' ? ' AND state = ?' : '') . ' LIMIT 60');
1032:                 $st->execute($state !== '' ? [$city, $state] : [$city]);
1033:                 foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $z) { $zips[] = trim((string) $z['zip']); if ($lat === null) { $lat = (float) $z['latitude']; $lon = (float) $z['longitude']; } }
1034:             }
1035:         } catch (Throwable) {}
1036:         if ($city === '' && $zip === '' && $lat === null) return null;
1037:         $label = $city !== '' ? ucwords(mb_strtolower($city)) . ($state !== '' ? ', ' . $state : '') : ($zip !== '' ? 'ZIP ' . $zip : 'Near you');
1038:         // Inside one of our areas, the device's place is that area's town, never "outside" (the launch plan's C.1;
1039:         // decisions/0060): the ZIPs it named, or for a bare point the nearest town's ZIP, read against our clusters, the
1040:         // same check a typed town or county gets (placeNamed, countyNamed). The shape is a town chosen in its area's.
1041:         $own = $zips;
1042:         if (!$own && $lat !== null && $lon !== null) {
1043:             try { require_once __DIR__ . '/Landmarks.php'; if ($nz = Landmarks::nearestZip($pdo, $lat, $lon)) $own = [$nz[0]]; } catch (Throwable) {}
1044:         }
1045:         $c = null;
1046:         try { if ($own) $c = self::clusterForZips($pdo, $own); } catch (Throwable) {}
1047:         if ($c) {
1048:             $p = ['label' => $label, 'sub' => 'Your place'] + array_diff_key(self::placeOut($pdo, 'cluster', (int) $c['id'], $label, (string) $c['slug'], $own, 'Your place'), ['label' => 1, 'sub' => 1])
1049:                + ['town' => $city !== '', 'area' => PlaceScope::publicName((string) $c['name']), 'city' => $city, 'state' => $state, 'outside' => false];
1050:             if ($lat !== null && $lon !== null) $p += ['lat' => $lat, 'lon' => $lon];
1051:             return $p;
1052:         }
1053:         $p = ['label' => $label, 'sub' => 'Your place', 'key' => '', 'level' => '', 'slug' => '', 'url' => '', 'zips' => $zips, 'city' => $city, 'state' => $state, 'outside' => true];
1054:         if ($lat !== null && $lon !== null) $p += ['lat' => $lat, 'lon' => $lon];
1055:         return $p;
1056:     }
1057: 
1058:     private static function placeFromScope(PDO $pdo, array $ctx): ?array
1059:     {
1060:         $level = (string) ($ctx['level'] ?? '');
1061:         $param = ['cluster' => 'cluster', 'geo_hub' => 'geo', 'hub' => 'hub'][$level] ?? null;
1062:         if (!$param || empty($ctx['slug'])) return self::loosePlace($pdo, $ctx);
1063:         $s = PlaceScope::resolve($pdo, [$param => (string) $ctx['slug']]);
1064:         if (!$s || !$s['found']) return self::loosePlace($pdo, $ctx);
1065:         $zips = [];
1066:         if ($level === 'cluster' && ($c = self::clusterRow($pdo, (int) $s['id']))) $zips = self::zipsOf($c);
1067:         // a town chosen in its town area ("Saint Johns, AZ" in The Greater St. Johns Area) is the place itself: its own
1068:         // ZIPs first; the rest of the area is "beyond", nearest first (decisions/0060)
1069:         if ($level === 'cluster' && $zips && preg_match('/^(.{2,60}),\s*([A-Z]{2})$/u', (string) ($ctx['town'] ?? ''), $tm)) {
1070:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT zip FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ? AND state = ?');
1071:             $st->execute([trim($tm[1]), $tm[2]]);
1072:             $tz = array_values(array_intersect(array_map(fn ($z) => trim((string) $z), $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN)), $zips));
1073:             if ($tz) return ['label' => trim($tm[1]) . ', ' . $tm[2], 'sub' => 'Your place', 'key' => '', 'level' => $level, 'slug' => (string) $ctx['slug'],
1074:                              'url' => self::placeOut($pdo, $level, (int) $s['id'], '', (string) $ctx['slug'], [], '')['url'], 'zips' => $tz, 'town' => true, 'area' => PlaceScope::publicName((string) $s['name'])];
1075:         }
1076:         return self::placeOut($pdo, $level, (int) $s['id'], PlaceScope::publicName((string) $s['name']), (string) $ctx['slug'], $zips, 'Your place');
1077:     }
1078: 
1079:     private static function findCategory(PDO $pdo, string $text): ?array
1080:     {
1081:         $cands = [$text, $text . 's', preg_replace('/s$/', '', $text)];
1082:         $toks = SearchText::tokens($text);
1083:         // an everyday word for one category ("pub" is Bars); a broad word ("sports": stadiums, gyms, golf…) stays words
1084:         if (count($toks) === 1 && count($alts = SearchText::alternatives($toks[0])) <= 3) foreach ($alts as $a) $cands[] = $a;
1085:         $cands = array_values(array_unique(array_filter($cands)));
1086:         $in = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($cands), '?'));
1087:         $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id, name, slug, parent_id FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND LOWER(name) IN (' . $in . ') ORDER BY parent_id IS NULL, id LIMIT 1');
1088:         $st->execute($cands);
1089:         $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
1090:         if (!$r) {
1091:             // its plain name, as visitors see it ("gyms & fitness centers", "self storage"): a name several categories share
1092:             // means the one with the most listings (decisions/0068)
1093:             $raw = [];
1094:             foreach (SearchText::plainNames($pdo) as $rn => $pn) if (in_array(mb_strtolower($pn), $cands, true)) $raw[] = $rn;
1095:             if ($raw) {
1096:                 $in2 = implode(',', array_fill(0, count($raw), '?'));
1097:                 $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT c.id, c.name, c.slug, c.parent_id, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM search_index si WHERE si.kind = 'listing' AND si.category_id = c.id) AS n FROM categories c WHERE c.is_active = 1 AND LOWER(c.name) IN ($in2) ORDER BY n DESC, c.id LIMIT 1");
1098:                 $st->execute($raw);
1099:                 $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
1100:             }
1101:         }
1102:         if (!$r) {
1103:             // the same words by their roots (therapy / therapists, pharmacy / pharmacies), same number of words
1104:             $root = fn (string $w) => strlen($w) > 5 ? preg_replace('/(ists?|ies|y|s)$/', '', $w) : preg_replace('/s$/', '', $w);
1105:             $mine = array_map($root, explode(' ', $text));
1106:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name, slug, parent_id FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND LOWER(name) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' ORDER BY parent_id IS NULL, id LIMIT 5");
1107:             $st->execute([SearchText::esc(implode(' ', $mine)) . '%']);
1108:             foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) {
1109:                 if (array_map($root, explode(' ', mb_strtolower((string) $c['name']))) === $mine) { $r = $c; break; }
1110:             }
1111:         }
1112:         if (!$r && mb_strlen($text) >= 4) {
1113:             // the name as shown, without its trade codes ("automobile parts & supplies" is "…Supplies-Retail-New")
1114:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT id, name, slug, parent_id FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND (LOWER(name) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!' OR LOWER(name) LIKE ? ESCAPE '!') ORDER BY parent_id IS NULL, id LIMIT 8");
1115:             $st->execute([SearchText::esc($text) . '-%', SearchText::esc($text) . ' (%']);
1116:             foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) if (mb_strtolower(SearchText::catLabel((string) $c['name'])) === $text) { $r = $c; break; }
1117:         }
1118:         return $r ? ['label' => (string) $r['name'], 'slug' => (string) $r['slug'], 'id' => (int) $r['id']] : null;
1119:     }
1120: 
1121:     // ---- results ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1122: 
1123:     /** OpenStreetMap's kinds that are places to go, not errands: a drive is near for them. */
1124:     private const OSM_GOING = ['trail', 'camping', 'park', 'viewpoint', 'todo', 'family', 'dog'];
1125: 
1126:     /**
1127:      * How far OpenStreetMap is asked, and ours are kept beside it, for "near me": 25 mi, or the Distance the person set when
1128:      * it's nearer and the kind is an errand (a café, a clinic); places to go keep the drive.
1129:      */
1130:     private static function osmReach(string $kind, array $ctx, bool $nearMe): int
1131:     {
1132:         $r = (string) ($ctx['reach'] ?? '');
1133:         return $nearMe && ctype_digit($r) && (int) $r > 0 && (int) $r < 25 && !in_array($kind, self::OSM_GOING, true) ? (int) $r : 25;
1134:     }
1135: 
1136:     /** "near me" needs at least this many places (ours and OpenStreetMap's) within 2 mi, else it offers 5; within 5, else it's lacking */
1137:     public const NEAR_ENOUGH = 3;
1138:     /** how near is near for "near me": errands (Jason's 2 mi, then 5) and the outdoors (a drive) */
1139:     public const NEAR_MILES = 5;
1140:     public const NEAR_MILES_OUTDOORS = 30;
1141: 
1142:     /** Where the person is (their point, else the place set), for telling apart towns of one name: Glendale, AZ to someone in Phoenix. */
1143:     private static ?array $near = null;
1144:     private static function mi(float $a, float $b, float $c, float $d): float
1145:     {
1146:         $r = M_PI / 180; $x = sin(($c - $a) * $r / 2) ** 2 + cos($a * $r) * cos($c * $r) * sin(($d - $b) * $r / 2) ** 2;
1147:         return 2 * 3958.8 * asin(min(1, sqrt($x)));
1148:     }
1149:     private static function nearFrom(PDO $pdo, array $ctx): ?array
1150:     {
1151:         if (is_numeric($ctx['lat'] ?? null) && is_numeric($ctx['lon'] ?? null)) return [(float) $ctx['lat'], (float) $ctx['lon']];
1152:         try { if (self::hasScope($ctx) && ($s = self::placeFromScope($pdo, $ctx)) && ($c = self::centerOf($pdo, $s))) return [(float) $c['lat'], (float) $c['lon']]; } catch (Throwable) {}
1153:         return null;
1154:     }
1155: 
1156:     public static function search(PDO $pdo, string $q, array $ctx = [], array $drop = [], ?int $viewer = null, int $radius = 0): array
1157:     {
1158:         // where the person is, for telling towns of one name apart; the words are searched as typed: a slip is offered as
1159:         // "Did you mean", never changed (decisions/0063, 0064; Jason, 2026-11-10: the auto-correct tried that day is rolled back)
1160:         self::$near = self::nearFrom($pdo, $ctx);
1161:         return self::searchAs($pdo, $q, $ctx, $drop, $viewer, $radius);
1162:     }
1163: 
1164:     private static function searchAs(PDO $pdo, string $q, array $ctx = [], array $drop = [], ?int $viewer = null, int $radius = 0): array
1165:     {
1166:         $u = self::understand($pdo, $q, $ctx, $drop);
1167:         // a word whose other meaning the words around it give ("copper tubing": pipe) isn't matched in descriptions, where it
1168:         // means the activity ("snow tubing" at a ski resort) (Jason, 2026-10-15)
1169:         $noAb = ['no_about' => array_values(array_filter(array_keys(self::ACT_NOT), fn ($w) => self::actNot($w, $q)))];
1170:         $chips = $u['chips'];
1171:         $text = $u['text'];
1172:         $chip = fn (string $t) => array_values(array_filter($chips, fn ($c) => $c['type'] === $t))[0] ?? null;
1173:         $place = $chip('place');
1174:         // "Where should I go" (decisions/0053 §4b): a distance around the place instead of its boundary.
1175:         $area = null;
1176:         // the Distance slider (decisions/0060): miles around the place instead of its boundary
1177:         $reach = $place ? (string) ($ctx['reach'] ?? 'all') : 'all';   // no place set: nothing to measure from
1178:         // "near me" (Jason, 2026-11-10): the nearest, with no distance cap ("Can't it find the nearest locations without a
1179:         // distance cap"), each with its miles; NEAR_MILES is the check, not a fence: fewer than NEAR_ENOUGH within it says our
1180:         // results are lacking (2 mi then 5 for errands, as first asked; 30 for the outdoors). No place: the panel asks first.
1181:         $nearMe = !empty($u['near_me']) || self::nearMe($q)['near'];
1182:         if ($place && $nearMe && empty($place['landmark'])) { $reach = '250'; $radius = 250; }
1183:         if ($radius <= 0 && ctype_digit($reach)) $radius = (int) $reach;
1184:         if ($radius <= 0 && !empty($place['landmark'])) $radius = 15;   // "near Lyman Lake": around it
1185:         if ($radius > 0 && $place && ($c = self::centerOf($pdo, $place))) {
1186:             $area = ['lat' => $c['lat'], 'lon' => $c['lon'], 'radius' => max(1, min(250, $radius))];
1187:             foreach ($chips as $i => $c) if ($c['type'] === 'place') $chips[$i]['radius'] = $area ? $area['radius'] : 0;
1188:         }
1189:         // an outing (2026-11-10): the kinds of places that suit who's coming, kept local: 25 mi around the place unless
1190:         // a distance was set ("Phoenix + 250 mi" sent the kids to a trailhead 47 mi off)
1191:         $outC = $chip('outing');
1192:         $outing = $outC && empty($outC['soft']) ? ['for' => (string) $outC['value'], 'age' => (string) ($outC['age'] ?? ''), 'setting' => (string) ($outC['setting'] ?? '')] : [];
1193:         if ($outing && $place && (!$area || $area['radius'] > 25) && !$nearMe && ($c = self::centerOf($pdo, $place))) {
1194:             $area = ['lat' => $c['lat'], 'lon' => $c['lon'], 'radius' => 25]; $radius = 25;
1195:             foreach ($chips as $i => $c0) if ($c0['type'] === 'place') $chips[$i]['radius'] = 25;
1196:             self::tr('Where', 'Within 25 mi', 'An outing stays local: the nearest that suit them.');
1197:         }
1198:         $cat = $chip('category');
1199:         $kind = $chip('kind')['value'] ?? null;
1200:         $conds = array_column(array_filter($chips, fn ($c) => $c['type'] === 'cond'), 'value');
1201:         $want = fn (string $k) => $kind === null || $kind === $k;
1202:         // When, Why, Who (decisions/0053 §8)
1203:         $whenC = $chip('when'); $whyC = $chip('why'); $whoC = $chip('who');
1204:         [$dow, $min] = isset($ctx['at']) && is_array($ctx['at']) ? $ctx['at'] : [(int) date('w'), (int) date('G') * 60 + (int) date('i')];
1205:         $when = [];
1206:         if ($whenC) {
1207:             $v = (string) $whenC['value'];
1208:             $when = $v === 'now' ? ['now' => [$dow, $min]] : ($v === 'tonight' ? ['now' => [$dow, 20 * 60]] : ($v === 'today' ? ['days' => [$dow]]
1209:                   : ($v === 'weekend' ? ['days' => [6, 0]] : (str_starts_with($v, 'day:') ? ['days' => [(int) substr($v, 4)]] : []))));
1210:         }
1211:         $why = (string) ($whyC['value'] ?? '');
1212:         // an action: the activity is what's searched, outdoor places first; the words left over put the best first
1213:         $doC = $chip('do'); $boost = []; $anyWord = false;
1214:         if ($doC) {
1215:             $boost = array_values(array_filter(preg_split('/\s+/u', $text) ?: [], fn ($w) => mb_strlen($w) >= 3
1216:                 && !in_array($w, array_merge(self::DESCRIBE, SearchIndex::STOP, ['where', 'what', 'when', 'how', 'can', 'could', 'should', 'would', 'go', 'going', 'take', 'kids', 'family', 'friends', 'today', 'tomorrow']), true)));
1217:             if ($boost) foreach ($chips as $i => $c) if ($c['type'] === 'do') $chips[$i]['about'] = $text;
1218:             if ($boost) self::tr('What', 'What it\'s for: "' . implode(' ', $boost) . '"', 'Places that mention it come first; it never hides the others.', $boost);
1219:             $text = self::ACT_WORDS[(string) $doC['value']] ?? (string) $doC['value'];
1220:             $anyWord = true;   // a trail, a trailhead or "hiking": any of the activity's words
1221:             $cat = null;
1222:             if ($why === '') $why = 'explore';
1223:         }
1224:         $aud = $whoC && empty($whoC['role']) ? (string) $whoC['value'] : '';

1226:         $fiveW = $why !== '' || $when || $whoC;
1227:         // One index for everything (decisions/0053 §8), once it has been built; until then, the kinds one by one.
1228:         $useIndex = class_exists('SearchIndex') && SearchIndex::ready($pdo) && SearchIndex::state($pdo, 'built_listing') !== '';
1229:         // the Relational Intent Map (decisions/0061): when the words name a situation, the rest of the results match
1230:         // whole words only ("broken down" isn't Downspouts or Downtown)
1231:         $sitA = $outing ? null : self::situationFor($pdo, $q, $place, $area, $reach, $ctx, $drop);
1232:         if ($sitA) self::tr('Why', 'A situation: ' . implode(' + ', $sitA['labels']), 'The words match ' . (count($sitA['phrases'] ?? []) === 1 ? 'the phrase' : 'the phrases') . 1234:         if ($sitA && $cat) {
1235:             $said0 = [];

') aren\'t things the directory knows.', $known ?: []); }   // only a real What beside a situation
