1783:     /** Ranked "Did you mean…?" searches, each with the situation it would answer when it names one. */
1784:     /**
1785:      * The kinds of place words mean (decisions/0067), with how many are in the place and in all: [['label', 'q', 'here',
1786:      * 'all', 'via']]. Only kinds that have something listed.
1787:      */
1788:     public static function meant(PDO $pdo, string $words, ?array $place, int $max = 4, array $also = []): array
1789:     {
1790:         if (!is_file(__DIR__ . '/Concepts.php')) return [];
1791:         require_once __DIR__ . '/Concepts.php';
1792:         // a town or a state's name ("washington") is a place, not a kind of business
1793:         if (self::isPlaceName($pdo, $words)) return [];
1794:         $ks = Concepts::kinds($pdo, $words, $max + 12);   // all of staff's kinds for the words; the best $max kept below
1795:         // what people who typed these words went on to open (decisions/0067; 0093, 2026-10-15): a hint only, never a ranking
1796:         // factor ("usage reinforces trends more than solves", and taps can be gamed). A kind the words lead to is labeled
1797:         // "most picked"; one they don't lead to isn't added at all. The meaning decides what's shown; clicks only label it.
1798:         $picked = [];
1799:         if (is_file(__DIR__ . '/Learning.php')) {
1800:             require_once __DIR__ . '/Learning.php';
1801:             try { $picked = Learning::picks($pdo, array_merge([$words], $also)); } catch (Throwable) {}
1802:         }
1803:         if ($picked) {
1804:             $have = array_column($ks, 'id');
1805:             try {
1806:                 $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT id, name FROM categories WHERE is_active = 1 AND id IN (' . implode(',', array_map('intval', array_keys($picked))) . ')');
1807:                 $st->execute();
1808:                 $nm = array_column($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC), 'name', 'id');
1809:             } catch (Throwable) { $nm = []; }
1810:             foreach ($picked as $cid => $n) {
1811:                 if (!isset($nm[$cid])) continue;
1812:                 $i = array_search($cid, $have, true);
1813:                 if ($i !== false) $ks[$i]['picked'] = $n;   // a label on what the words already mean; nothing added
1814:             }
1815:         }
1816:         $out = [];
1817:         foreach ($ks as $k) {
1818:             $p = [':cid' => (int) $k['id']];
1819:             $here = $place ? SearchIndex::whereSql($pdo, $place, null, $p) : '';
1820:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n" . ($here !== '' ? ", SUM(CASE WHEN $here THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS h" : ', 0 AS h') . " FROM search_index si WHERE si.kind = 'listing' AND si.category_id = :cid");
1821:             $st->execute($p);
1822:             $r = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) ?: ['n' => 0, 'h' => 0];
1823:             if ((int) $r['n'] === 0) continue;
1824:             $lab = SearchText::catLabel((string) $k['name']);
1825:             // two categories that read the same ("Beer & Ale" retail and wholesale): one kind, both counted
1826:             $dup = array_search(mb_strtolower($lab), array_map(fn ($o) => mb_strtolower($o['label']), $out), true);
1827:             if ($dup !== false) { $out[$dup]['here'] += (int) $r['h']; $out[$dup]['all'] += (int) $r['n']; $out[$dup]['ids'][] = (int) $k['id']; $out[$dup]['sure'] = $out[$dup]['sure'] || (bool) $k['sure']; continue; }
1828:             $out[] = ['label' => $lab, 'q' => mb_strtolower($lab), 'here' => (int) $r['h'], 'all' => (int) $r['n'], 'via' => (string) $k['via'], 'sure' => (bool) $k['sure'],
1829:                       'ids' => [(int) $k['id']], 'picked' => (int) ($k['picked'] ?? 0), 'score' => round((float) $k['score'], 2)];
1830:         }
1831:         // what people opened first; then the sure ones, then the maybes; in each, the ones in the place first, keeping the
1832:         // meaning's order otherwise
1833:         // the kind named by the words first, then the meaning's order; among equals (staff's words give several kinds at
1834:         // once: "mental health"), the most listed
1835:         $named = fn ($o) => str_contains(mb_strtolower($o['label']), mb_strtolower(trim($words)));
1836:         usort($out, fn ($a, $b) => [$b['sure'], $b['here'] > 0, $named($b), $b['score'], $b['here'], $b['all']] <=> [$a['sure'], $a['here'] > 0, $named($a), $a['score'], $a['here'], $a['all']]);
1837:         return array_slice($out, 0, $max);
1838:     }
1839: 
1840:     /** Whether the words are a town's or a state's name ("washington", "show low"). */
1841:     public static function isPlaceName(PDO $pdo, string $words): bool
1842:     {
1843:         $wl = trim(mb_strtolower($words));
1844:         if (isset(self::STATES[$wl])) return true;
1845:         try { $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT 1 FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ? LIMIT 1'); $st->execute([$wl]); return (bool) $st->fetchColumn(); } catch (Throwable) { return false; }
1846:     }
1847: 
1848:     /** Listings and places whose description mentions the words, in the place first: the last resort before "nothing". */
1849:     private static function mentions(PDO $pdo, string $words, ?array $place): array
1850:     {
1851:         $ws = array_values(array_filter(preg_split('/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u', mb_strtolower($words)) ?: [], fn ($w) => mb_strlen($w) >= 4));
1852:         if (!$ws) return [];
1853:         $p = [];
1854:         $or = [];
1855:         foreach (array_slice($ws, 0, 3) as $i => $w) {
1856:             $stem = preg_replace('/(es|s)$/', '', $w);
1857:             $or[] = "(CONCAT(' ', LOWER(si.about), ' ') LIKE :m$i OR CONCAT(' ', LOWER(si.terms), ' ') LIKE :n$i)";
1858:             $p[":m$i"] = '% ' . SearchText::esc($stem) . '%'; $p[":n$i"] = '% ' . SearchText::esc($stem) . '%';
1859:         }
1860:         $here = $place ? SearchIndex::whereSql($pdo, $place, null, $p) : '';
1861:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT si.kind, si.item_id, si.label, si.sub, si.url" . ($here !== '' ? ", COALESCE($here, 0) AS here" : ', 0 AS here')
1862:             . " FROM search_index si WHERE si.kind IN ('listing', 'outdoors', 'story', 'experience') AND (" . implode(' OR ', $or) . ") ORDER BY here DESC, si.weight DESC LIMIT 5");
1863:         $st->execute($p);
1864:         return array_map(fn ($r) => ['label' => (string) $r['label'], 'sub' => SearchText::catLabel((string) $r['sub']), 'url' => (string) $r['url'], 'kind' => (string) $r['kind'],
1865:                                      'id' => (string) $r['item_id'], 'here' => (int) $r['here'] === 1, 'peek' => $r['kind'] === 'listing'], $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC));
1866:     }
1867: 
1868:     /** Offers for words nothing knows: [['q' => the words with each fixed, 'fixed' => [[typed, offered]]]], the likeliest first. */
1869:     private static function offers(PDO $pdo, string $q): array
1870:     {
1871:         if (!is_file(__DIR__ . '/Spelling.php')) return [];
1872:         require_once __DIR__ . '/Spelling.php';
1873:         $ws = preg_split('/\s+/u', mb_strtolower(trim($q))) ?: [];
1874:         $vocab = self::whatVocab($pdo); $out = [];
1875:         foreach ($ws as $i => $w) {
1876:             if (mb_strlen($w) < 4 || !preg_match('/^[a-z]+$/', $w) || isset($vocab[self::root($w)]) || self::nameHas($pdo, $w)) continue;
1877:             $cands = [];
1878:             if (mb_strlen($w) >= 6) for ($k = 3, $n = mb_strlen($w); $k <= $n - 3; $k++) {   // run together: the kind of place it makes
1879:                 $a = mb_substr($w, 0, $k); $b = mb_substr($w, $k);
1880:                 if (Spelling::known($a) && Spelling::known($b) && self::catHas($pdo, $a . ' ' . $b)) { $cands[] = $a . ' ' . $b; break; }
1881:             }
1882:             if (!Spelling::known($w)) foreach (Spelling::near($w, 3) as $c) if (isset($vocab[self::root($c)]) || self::catHas($pdo, $c)) $cands[] = $c;
1883:             // a town's name a letter off ("flagstaf": Flagstaff), from the locations data: offered, never read as the place
1884:             // on its own (decisions/0063, 0064)
1885:             if (!$cands && !Spelling::known($w) && ($tn = self::townSlip($pdo, $w)) !== null) $cands[] = $tn;
1886:             foreach (array_slice(array_values(array_unique($cands)), 0, 3) as $c) {
1887:                 $f = $ws; $f[$i] = $c;
1888:                 $out[] = ['q' => implode(' ', $f), 'score' => 0, 'fixed' => [[$w, $c]]];
1889:             }
1890:             if ($out) break;   // the first word that needs help
1891:         }
1892:         return $out;
1893:     }
1894: 
1895:     /**
1896:      * The town a word is a letter from (one added, missing, wrong or two swapped: "flagstaf", "sedonna"), in the pilot
1897:      * states first, then the most people; or null. Only names that start the same way are compared (the ZIP list's index).
1898:      */
1899:     private static function townSlip(PDO $pdo, string $w): ?string
1900:     {
1901:         $w = mb_strtolower(trim($w));
1902:         if (mb_strlen($w) < 5 || !preg_match('/^[a-z]+$/', $w)) return null;
1903:         try {
1904:             require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
1905:             $in = "'" . implode("','", NpiBulk::STATES) . "'";
1906:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT LOWER(TRIM(city)) AS city FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city LIKE ? GROUP BY TRIM(city), TRIM(state) ORDER BY (TRIM(state) IN ($in)) DESC, SUM(COALESCE(population, 0)) DESC LIMIT 300");
1907:             $st->execute([SearchText::esc(mb_substr($w, 0, 3)) . '%']);
1908:             foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_COLUMN) as $c) {
1909:                 $c = (string) $c;
1910:                 if ($c === $w || !preg_match('/^[a-z]+$/', $c)) continue;
1911:                 $swap = false;
1912:                 if (strlen($c) === strlen($w)) for ($i = 0; $i + 1 < strlen($w); $i++) if ($w === substr($c, 0, $i) . $c[$i + 1] . $c[$i] . substr($c, $i + 2)) { $swap = true; break; }
1913:                 if ($swap || levenshtein($w, $c) === 1) return $c;
1914:             }
1915:         } catch (Throwable) {}
1916:         return null;
1917:     }
1918: 
1919:     /** The same spelling offers as this search's, for the directory's own search (2026-11-12): [['q', …]]. */
1920:     public static function spellOffers(PDO $pdo, string $q): array { return self::didYouMean($pdo, $q); }
1921: 
1922:     private static function didYouMean(PDO $pdo, string $q, bool $typing = false): array
1923:     {
1924:         if (is_file(__DIR__ . '/Situations.php')) require_once __DIR__ . '/Situations.php';
1925:         if (is_file(__DIR__ . '/WordSenses.php')) require_once __DIR__ . '/WordSenses.php';
1926:         $out = SearchText::suggestions($pdo, $q, 5, $typing);
1927:         // only words that fit where the typed one sits (decisions/0063): no noun where a verb belongs ("how to compost"
1928:         // isn't "how to compass"), and none a person removed for this search
1929:         require_once __DIR__ . '/Lexicon.php'; require_once __DIR__ . '/SuggestionFlags.php';
1930:         $tok = Lexicon::tokens($q);
1931:         $out = array_values(array_filter($out, function ($d) use ($pdo, $tok) {
1932:             foreach ($d['fixed'] as [$typed, $cand]) {
1933:                 // a real word is never "corrected" (decisions/0064): "prepare" isn't a slip for "propane"
1934:                 require_once __DIR__ . '/WordFamilies.php';
1935:                 if (WordFamilies::partsOf((string) $typed) !== '' || WordFamilies::partsOf(WordFamilies::singular((string) $typed)) !== '') return false;   // "hills" too, not only "hill"
1936:                 if (is_file(__DIR__ . '/LocalWords.php')) { require_once __DIR__ . '/LocalWords.php'; if (LocalWords::knows((string) $typed)) return false; }   // a word used here (decisions/0157)
1937:                 $i = array_search($typed, $tok, true); if ($i !== false && !Lexicon::fitsSlot($pdo, $tok, (int) $i, (string) $cand)) return false;
1938:             }
1939:             return true;
1940:         }));
1941:         // never neither (decisions/0064: "a slip still gets help"; Jason, 2026-11-10: "Fixed or spelling offered"): a word
1942:         // nothing knows with no offer yet gets the closest words our directory uses, and words run together the kind of
1943:         // place they make ("icecream": ice cream). Offered only, never changed.
1944:         if (!$out) $out = self::offers($pdo, $q);
1945:         // two real words a letter from a thing ("seal lions": sea lions): offered first
1946:         if (($ps = self::phraseSlip($q)) && !in_array($ps['q'], array_column($out, 'q'), true)) array_unshift($out, $ps);
1947:         $out = array_slice(SuggestionFlags::filter($pdo, $q, $out, 'q'), 0, 3);
1948:         foreach ($out as &$d) {
1949:             $d['situation'] = null;
1950:             if (class_exists('Situations')) { try { $s = Situations::read($pdo, $d['q']); if ($s) $d['situation'] = (string) $s[0]['label']; } catch (Throwable) {} }
1951:         }
1952:         unset($d);
1953:         return $out;
1954:     }

2012:     /** Words that ask for the platform's own help, typed alone ("help", "hel", "support"). */
2013:     private const PLATFORM_HELP = ['help', 'help me', 'help center', 'support', 'i need help', 'need help'];
2014: 
2015:     /**
2016:      * The question back when "help" stands alone: help using Traversence, or help near the place.
2017:      * $prefix: the words may be on their way to it ("hel"). ['q', 'options' => [['label', 'url'|'q']]] or null.
2018:      */
2019:     public static function askHelp(string $q, ?array $place, bool $prefix = false): ?array
2020:     {
2021:         $q = trim(mb_strtolower($q));
2022:         $hit = false;
2023:         foreach (self::PLATFORM_HELP as $w) if ($q === $w || ($prefix && mb_strlen($q) >= 3 && str_starts_with($w, $q))) { $hit = true; break; }
2024:         if (!$hit) return null;
2025:         $where = $place ? PlaceScope::publicName((string) $place['label']) : '';
2026:         return ['q' => 'Are you looking for…', 'options' => [
2027:             ['label' => 'Help using Traversence', 'sub' => 'Help Center and FAQ', 'url' => '/user/dashboard.php#help'],
2028:             ['label' => 'Help ' . ($where !== '' ? 'in ' . $where : 'near you'), 'sub' => 'Assistance, services and support', 'q' => 'get help' . ($where !== '' ? ' in ' . $where : '')],
2029:         ]];
2030:     }
2031: 
2032:     /**
2033:      * A place on its own (the search names only where): what would you like to know about it? Each choice is a search
2034:      * there; "Make it my place" changes the set location (only when they choose to get local). A town whose name is used
2035:      * in other states ("springfield") also offers those: "Not this one?". ['kind' => 'place', 'q', 'options', 'alts']
2036:      */
2037:     public static function placeCard(PDO $pdo, array $place, string $typed = ''): ?array
2038:     {
2039:         $label = trim((string) ($place['label'] ?? ''));
2040:         if ($label === '') return null;
2041:         $short = trim(explode('·', $label)[0]);
2042:         if (!empty($place['is_state'])) $short .= ' state';   // "things to do in washington state", never Washington, DC
2043:         $opts = [
2044:             ['label' => 'Things to do', 'sub' => 'Outdoors, culture, places to go', 'q' => 'things to do in ' . $short],
2045:             ['label' => 'Places to eat', 'sub' => 'Restaurants, cafés, local food', 'q' => 'restaurants in ' . $short],
2046:             ['label' => 'Places to stay', 'sub' => 'Hotels, lodges, camping', 'q' => 'places to stay in ' . $short],
2047:             ['label' => 'Moving there', 'sub' => 'Movers, storage, utilities, places to live', 'q' => 'moving to ' . $short],
2048:             ['label' => 'Plan a trip', 'sub' => 'Where, when, how long and what', 'url' => '/discovery/?view=plan' . (!empty($place['slug']) && ($pp = ['cluster' => 'cluster', 'geo_hub' => 'geo', 'hub' => 'hub'][$place['level'] ?? ''] ?? null) ? '&' . $pp . '=' . rawurlencode((string) $place['slug']) : '')],
2049:         ];
2050:         // get local: make it the set place (in our areas, the area; outside them, the town or county itself)
2051:         $set = !empty($place['slug']) ? ['scope' => ['level' => (string) $place['level'], 'slug' => (string) $place['slug'], 'label' => $short]]
2052:              : array_filter(['city' => (string) ($place['city'] ?? ''), 'state' => (string) ($place['state'] ?? ''), 'zip' => empty($place['city']) && !empty($place['zips']) ? (string) $place['zips'][0] : '']);
2053:         if ($set) $opts[] = ['label' => 'Make it my place', 'sub' => 'Get local: search here by default', 'set' => $set + ['label' => $short]];
2054:         // the same town name in other states, when no state was typed
2055:         $alts = [];
2056:         if (!empty($place['city']) && !empty($place['state']) && !preg_match('/\b' . preg_quote(mb_strtolower((string) $place['state']), '/') . '\b|' . preg_quote(mb_strtolower((string) (array_search($place['state'], self::STATES, true) ?: '#')), '/') . '/u', mb_strtolower($typed))) {
2057:             try {
2058:                 $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(state) AS state, COUNT(*) AS n FROM zip_coordinates WHERE city = ? AND state <> ? GROUP BY TRIM(state) ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 6');
2059:                 $st->execute([(string) $place['city'], (string) $place['state']]);
2060:                 foreach ($st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $alts[] = ['label' => ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $place['city'])) . ', ' . $r['state'], 'q' => mb_strtolower((string) $place['city']) . ', ' . mb_strtolower((string) $r['state'])];
2061:             } catch (Throwable) {}
2062:         }
2063:         return ['kind' => 'place', 'q' => 'What would you like to know about ' . $short . '?', 'options' => $opts, 'alts' => $alts, 'place' => $short];
2064:     }
2065: 
2066:     /** Towns by that name in several states, when the words are only a name: ['kind' => 'place', 'q', 'options'] or null. */
2067:     public static function whichPlace(PDO $pdo, string $text): ?array
2068:     {
2069:         $t = trim(mb_strtolower($text));
2070:         if ($t === '' || substr_count($t, ' ') > 2 || mb_strlen($t) < 3) return null;
2071:         try {
2072:             $t1 = preg_replace(['/^st\.? /', '/^mt\.? /', '/^ft\.? /'], ['saint ', 'mount ', 'fort '], $t);
2073:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(state) AS state, TRIM(city) AS city, COUNT(*) AS n FROM zip_coordinates WHERE ' . self::squashSql('city') . ' = ? GROUP BY TRIM(state), TRIM(city) ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 8');
2074:             $st->execute([self::squash($t1)]);   // whatever the punctuation or spacing ("coeur d alene")
2075:             $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
2076:         } catch (Throwable) { return null; }
2077:         $sp = isset(self::STATES[$t]) ? self::stateNamed($pdo, self::STATES[$t]) : null;   // "washington": the state first
2078:         if (!$rows) return $sp ? self::placeCard($pdo, $sp, $text) : null;
2079:         // one town by that name ("seattle"): its place card straight away
2080:         if (count($rows) === 1 && !$sp && ($p1 = self::placeNamed($pdo, mb_strtolower((string) $rows[0]['city']), (string) $rows[0]['state']))) return self::placeCard($pdo, $p1, $text . ' ' . $rows[0]['state']);
2081:         $name = ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $rows[0]['city']));
2082:         $opts = array_map(fn ($r) => ['label' => $name . ', ' . $r['state'], 'sub' => (array_search($r['state'], self::STATES, true) ? ucwords((string) array_search($r['state'], self::STATES, true)) : $r['state']), 'q' => mb_strtolower($name) . ', ' . mb_strtolower((string) $r['state'])], $rows);
2083:         if ($sp) array_unshift($opts, ['label' => $sp['label'] . ' (the state)', 'sub' => 'State', 'q' => $t . ' state']);
2084:         return ['kind' => 'place', 'q' => count($rows) > 1 ? 'Which ' . $name . '?' : 'Did you mean ' . $name . ', ' . $rows[0]['state'] . '?', 'options' => $opts, 'alts' => []];
2085:     }
2086: 
2087:     /** After Enter: a tool the words ask for, offered above the results ("planning a trip" → the Trip Planner). */
2088:     private static function askTool(string $q, ?array $place): ?array
2089:     {
2090:         $opts = [];
2091:         foreach (self::tools($q, $place) as $t) if ($t['lead']) $opts[] = ['label' => $t['item']['label'], 'sub' => preg_replace('/^Tool · /', '', $t['item']['crumb']), 'url' => $t['item']['url']];
2092:         return $opts ? ['q' => 'Are you looking for…', 'options' => $opts] : null;
2093:     }
2094: 
2095:     /**
2096:      * The site's own tools, offered when the words ask for one ("planning", "plan a trip", "road trip" → the Trip Planner).
2097:      * 'strong' words always mean the tool; 'words' mean it when they are most of the search ("planning", "my plans"), and
2098:      * otherwise it's offered after what's in the place ("planning and zoning" is still the office).
2099:      */
2100:     private const TOOLS = [
2101:         'trip-planner' => ['label' => 'Trip Planner', 'sub' => 'Plan a trip: where, when, how long and what you\'re after', 'url' => '/discovery/?view=plan',
2102:             'strong' => ['trip', 'trips', 'planner', 'itinerary', 'itineraries', 'vacation', 'vacations', 'getaway', 'getaways', 'roadtrip', 'holiday', 'holidays'],
2103:             'words' => ['plan', 'plans', 'planning', 'planned', 'visit', 'visiting', 'travel', 'traveling', 'travelling']],
2104:     ];
2105:     private const TOOL_FILL = ['a', 'an', 'the', 'my', 'our', 'to', 'for', 'me', 'i', 'we', 'help', 'with', 'road', 'day', 'weekend', 'family', 'next', 'this', 'up'];
2106: 
2107:     /** Tools the words ask for: [['item' => suggestion row, 'lead' => bool]]. $prefix: the last word may still be being typed. */
2108:     public static function tools(string $q, ?array $place, bool $prefix = false): array
2109:     {
2110:         $toks = preg_split('/[^\p{L}0-9]+/u', mb_strtolower($q), -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY);
2111:         if (!$toks) return [];
2112:         $out = [];
2113:         foreach (self::TOOLS as $t) {
2114:             $all = array_merge($t['strong'], $t['words']);
2115:             $strong = false; $any = false; $rest = 0;
2116:             foreach ($toks as $i => $w) {
2117:                 $last = $prefix && $i === count($toks) - 1 && mb_strlen($w) >= 4;
2118:                 $isS = in_array($w, $t['strong'], true) || ($last && array_filter($t['strong'], fn ($k) => str_starts_with($k, $w)));
2119:                 $isW = in_array($w, $t['words'], true) || ($last && array_filter($all, fn ($k) => str_starts_with($k, $w)));
2120:                 if ($isS) $strong = true;
2121:                 if ($isS || $isW) $any = true;
2122:                 elseif (!in_array($w, self::TOOL_FILL, true)) $rest++;
2123:             }
2124:             if (!$any) continue;
2125:             $url = $t['url'];
2126:             if ($place && !empty($place['level']) && !empty($place['slug'])) $url .= '&' . (['cluster' => 'cluster', 'geo_hub' => 'geo', 'hub' => 'hub'][$place['level']] ?? 'cluster') . '=' . rawurlencode((string) $place['slug']);
2127:             $out[] = ['item' => ['label' => $t['label'], 'crumb' => 'Tool · ' . $t['sub'], 'url' => $url, 'kind' => 'tool', 'here' => true], 'lead' => $strong || $rest === 0];
2128:         }
2129:         return $out;
2130:     }
2131: 
2132:     /**
2133:      * Direct hits for what's typed so far: names and categories that start with it, in the person's place first, then
2134:      * beyond it, nearest first. Each has a place › kind line and opens its page. The place someone set isn't changed.
2135:      * ['items' => [['label', 'crumb', 'url', 'kind', 'here', 'peek'?, 'id'?]], 'here' => bool any in the place, 'place' => label]
2136:      */
2137:     /**
2138:      * How well a suggestion's name matches what was typed: 3 the whole name ("phoenix": Phoenix), 2 the typed words whole
2139:      * at its start ("four corners": Four Corners Monument), 1 plus the share typed when it only starts that way
2140:      * ("phoenix" is 7 of Phoenixville's 12 letters: 1.58), 0 otherwise (a kind it means). One for the place you're in
2141:      * ("plum" in Show Low: Plumbers) adds 1.
2142:      */
2143:     public static function matchScore(string $label, string $q, bool $here = false, string $kind = '', string $track = ''): float
2144:     {
2145:         $name = trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]+/u', ' ', mb_strtolower(explode(',', explode(' · ', $label)[0])[0]))));
2146:         $typed = trim((string) preg_replace('/\s+/u', ' ', preg_replace('/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]+/u', ' ', mb_strtolower($q))));
2147:         if ($name === '' || $typed === '') return 0.0;
2148:         if ($name === $typed) $s = 3.0;
2149:         elseif (str_starts_with($name . ' ', $typed . ' ')) $s = 2.0;
2150:         elseif (str_starts_with($name, $typed)) $s = 1.0 + mb_strlen($typed) / max(1, mb_strlen($name));
2151:         else $s = 0.0;
2152:         // the page decides what leads, among names that match (the Chameleon Filter, decisions/0058 §19; Jason, 2026-11-11):
2153:         // places, the outdoors, stories and culture on Let's Explore; businesses and their kinds on Get Local
2154:         $fits = $track === 'explore' ? in_array($kind, ['place', 'outdoors', 'story', 'group', 'knowledge', 'tag', 'experience', 'culture'], true)
2155:               : ($track === 'local' ? in_array($kind, ['listing', 'category', 'food'], true) : false);
2156:         return $s + ($here && $s > 0 ? 1.0 : 0.0) + ($fits && $s > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.0);
2157:     }
2158: 
2159:     /**
2160:      * Suggestions in order of matchScore, the given order kept among equals. "Here" counts only with a place set or named:
2161:      * with none, every kind of business was "here" and "pho" led with Photographers over Phoenix (2026-11-11).
2162:      */
2163:     private static function byMatch(array $items, string $q, string $track = '', bool $placed = true): array
2164:     {
2165:         $k = [];
2166:         foreach (array_values($items) as $i => $it) $k[] = [self::matchScore((string) ($it['label'] ?? ''), $q, $placed && !empty($it['here']), (string) ($it['kind'] ?? ''), $track), $i, $it];
2167:         usort($k, fn ($a, $b) => [$b[0], $a[1]] <=> [$a[0], $b[1]]);
2168:         return array_column($k, 2);
2169:     }
2170: 
2171:     /**
2172:      * What a person may want with a thing the words name (Jason, 2026-11-11: "Sea Lions is a thing so what information would
2173:      * someone be wanting? To see, to care for, to learn more, instead a blank search"). By what the thing is, from WordNet
2174:      * (WordFamilies: creature, pet, food…; else word-senses.php: p plant, o natural object), never a word list of our own:
2175:      * [label, what it finds, the search it runs]. {pl} and {sg} are the thing, plural and singular; {w} as typed (food and
2176:      * medicine are often counted as one: "ice cream", never "ice creams").
2177:      */
2178:     private const THING_WAYS = [
2179:         'creature' => [['Where to see {pl}', 'places that mention them: wildlife areas, parks, preserves', '{pl}'],
2180:                        ['Wildlife sanctuaries, zoos and aquariums', 'to see {pl} up close', 'wildlife sanctuaries'],
2181:                        ['Help a hurt or stranded animal', 'animal rescue and wildlife rehab', 'animal rescue'],
2182:                        ['Learn about {pl}', 'museums and nature centers', 'museums']],
2183:         'pet' => [['Adopt a {sg}', 'animal shelters and adoptions', 'animal shelters'], ['Care for a {sg}', 'veterinarians, grooming and boarding', 'veterinarians'],
2184:                   ['Pet supplies', 'food and gear for a {sg}', 'pet supplies']],
2185:         'food' => [['Where to eat {w}', 'restaurants and places that serve it', '{w}'], ['Where to buy {w}', 'grocery stores and markets', 'grocery stores']],
2186:         'vehicle' => [['{Sg} repair', 'shops that fix them', '{sg} repair'], ['{Sg} rental', 'rent one', '{sg} rental'], ['{Sg} dealers', 'buy one', '{sg} dealers']],
2187:         'device' => [['{Sg} repair', 'shops that fix them', '{sg} repair'], ['Where to buy a {sg}', 'stores that sell them', '{sg}']],
2188:         'meds' => [['Pharmacies', 'where to get {w}', 'pharmacy']],
2189:         'p' => [['Where to see {pl}', 'gardens, preserves and trails that mention them', '{pl}'], ['Gardens', 'to see {pl} growing', 'gardens'], ['Garden centers', 'to buy {pl}', 'garden centers']],
2190:         'o' => [['Where to see {pl}', 'the outdoors and places that mention them', '{pl}']],
2191:     ];
2192: 
2193:     /**
2194:      * The thing the words name and what it is: ['words' as typed, 'sg', 'pl', 'what' => a THING_WAYS key], or null. A
2195:      * two- or three-word thing anywhere in the words ("sea lion preserves": sea lion); one word only when it's all there is.
2196:      */
2197:     private static function thingIn(string $q): ?array
2198:     {
2199:         require_once __DIR__ . '/WordFamilies.php';
2200:         $ws = array_values(array_filter(preg_split('/\s+/u', trim(mb_strtolower($q))) ?: [], fn ($w) => preg_match('/^[a-z\'-]+$/', $w)));
2201:         if (!$ws || count($ws) > 4) return null;
2202:         $whatOf = function (string $ph): string {
2203:             $f = (string) WordFamilies::family($ph);
2204:             if (isset(self::THING_WAYS[$f])) return $f;
2205:             $sn = SearchText::thingSense($ph);
2206:             if ($sn === '' && !str_contains($ph, ' ')) { static $sx = null; if ($sx === null) { $fx = __DIR__ . '/../data/word-senses.php'; $sx = is_file($fx) ? (array) require $fx : []; } $sn = (string) ($sx[$ph] ?? $sx[WordFamilies::singular($ph)] ?? ''); }
2207:             $k = $sn !== '' ? (str_contains($sn, 'f') ? 'food' : $sn[0]) : '';   // a food among its senses reads as food (jalapeno, not the plant)
2208:             return isset(self::THING_WAYS[$k]) ? $k : '';
2209:         };
2210:         $found = null;
2211:         foreach ([3, 2] as $len) for ($i = 0; $i + $len <= count($ws) && !$found; $i++) {
2212:             $ph = implode(' ', array_slice($ws, $i, $len));
2213:             if (SearchText::thingSense($ph) === '' && WordFamilies::family($ph) === null) continue;
2214:             if (($k = $whatOf($ph)) !== '') $found = [$ph, $k];
2215:         }
2216:         if (!$found && count($ws) === 1 && mb_strlen($ws[0]) >= 3 && ($k = $whatOf($ws[0])) !== '') $found = [$ws[0], $k];
2217:         if (!$found) return null;
2218:         [$ph, $k] = $found;
2219:         // a food named for an animal is its food ("dog food", "cat food"): the pet's ways (supplies, vets), not a restaurant
2220:         if ($k === 'food' && str_contains($ph, ' ') && in_array(WordFamilies::family(explode(' ', $ph)[0]), ['pet', 'creature'], true)) { $k = 'pet'; $ph = explode(' ', $ph)[0]; }
2221:         $parts = explode(' ', $ph); $last = (string) end($parts);
2222:         $sgLast = WordFamilies::singular($last);
2223:         $sg = implode(' ', array_merge(array_slice($parts, 0, -1), [$sgLast]));
2224:         if ($sgLast !== $last) $pl = $ph;
2225:         else {
2226:             $plLast = preg_match('/[^aeiou]y$/', $last) ? mb_substr($last, 0, -1) . 'ies' : (preg_match('/(s|x|z|ch|sh)$/', $last) ? $last . 'es' : $last . 's');
2227:             $pl = implode(' ', array_merge(array_slice($parts, 0, -1), [$plLast]));
2228:         }
2229:         return ['words' => $ph, 'sg' => $sg, 'pl' => $pl, 'what' => $k];
2230:     }
2231: 
2232:     /** The ways to a thing the words name, as intents: [['label', 'means', 'q', 'kind' => 'thing']]. */
2233:     private static function thingWays(string $q): array
2234:     {
2235:         $t = self::thingIn($q);
2236:         if (!$t) return [];
2237:         $fill = fn (string $s) => strtr($s, ['{pl}' => $t['pl'], '{sg}' => $t['sg'], '{w}' => $t['words'], '{Sg}' => ucfirst($t['sg']), '{Pl}' => ucfirst($t['pl'])]);
2238:         return array_map(fn ($w) => ['label' => ucfirst($fill($w[0])), 'means' => $fill($w[1]), 'q' => $fill($w[2]), 'kind' => 'thing'], self::THING_WAYS[$t['what']]);
2239:     }
2240: 
2241:     /**
2242:      * Two real words, one letter from a thing ("seal lions": sea lions), offered, never changed (decisions/0064 note,
2243:      * 2026-11-11). Only the first word of the pair is read as the slip, so the thing the person named stays theirs ("red car"
2244:      * is never a "red cap"). ['q', 'score', 'fixed' => [[typed, offered]]], or null.
2245:      */
2246:     public static function phraseSlip(string $q): ?array
2247:     {
2248:         $ws = preg_split('/\s+/u', trim(mb_strtolower($q))) ?: [];
2249:         for ($i = 0; $i + 1 < count($ws); $i++) {
2250:             [$a, $b] = [$ws[$i], $ws[$i + 1]];
2251:             if (!preg_match('/^[a-z]{3,}$/', $a) || !preg_match('/^[a-z]{3,}$/', $b) || SearchText::thingSense("$a $b") !== '') continue;
2252:             $n = strlen($a); $cands = [];
2253:             for ($k = 0; $k < $n; $k++) $cands[] = substr($a, 0, $k) . substr($a, $k + 1);   // a letter too many (seal: sea)
2254:             for ($k = 0; $k + 1 < $n; $k++) $cands[] = substr($a, 0, $k) . $a[$k + 1] . $a[$k] . substr($a, $k + 2);   // two swapped
2255:             foreach (range('a', 'z') as $ch) {
2256:                 for ($k = 0; $k < $n; $k++) $cands[] = substr($a, 0, $k) . $ch . substr($a, $k + 1);   // one wrong
2257:                 for ($k = 0; $k <= $n; $k++) $cands[] = substr($a, 0, $k) . $ch . substr($a, $k);   // one missing
2258:             }
2259:             foreach ($cands as $c) {
2260:                 if ($c === $a || strlen($c) < 2 || SearchText::thingSense("$c $b") === '') continue;
2261:                 $f = $ws; $f[$i] = $c;
2262:                 return ['q' => implode(' ', $f), 'score' => 0, 'fixed' => [[$a, $c]]];
2263:             }
2264:         }
2265:         return null;
2266:     }
2267: 
2268:     /**
2269:      * The intents a word being typed may serve, as full words with their context (Jason, 2026-11-11: "Partial word,
2270:      * suggests a full word, that suggestion has the context matching potential intent"; "What we hold seems to be a gate
2271:      * to information"; "Navajo, Hopi, and Apache are words first"). From sources, not from what we hold: a country or
2272:      * state and its people (app/data/demonyms.php, from Wikidata: Mexican, Mexico), a tribal nation (the nations table,
2273:      * its public side only, decisions/0066), the U.S. town or county it names. A people or a nation brings its food and its
2274:      * culture; what we hold only orders and fills the results. [['label', 'means', 'q', 'kind']], places first.
2275:      */
2276:     private static function intents(PDO $pdo, string $q, array $done): array
2277:     {
2278:         $tk = preg_split('/\s+/u', mb_strtolower(trim($q))) ?: [];
2279:         $code = mb_strlen(trim($q)) === 2 && in_array(strtoupper(trim($q)), self::STATES, true);   // "pa": a state's code
2280:         if (count($tk) > 2 || (mb_strlen(trim($q)) < 3 && !$code) || !preg_match('/^[\p{L} ]+$/u', trim(mb_strtolower($q)))) return [];
2281:         $typed = trim(mb_strtolower($q));
2282:         static $dem = null;
2283:         if ($dem === null) { $f = __DIR__ . '/../data/demonyms.php'; $dem = is_file($f) ? (array) require $f : ['peoples' => [], 'places' => []]; }
2284:         require_once __DIR__ . '/crawler/NpiBulk.php';
2285:         $abbr = array_flip(self::STATES);
2286:         $places = []; $peoples = []; $seen = [];
2287:         $starts = fn (string $name) => (bool) preg_match('/(^| )' . preg_quote($typed, '/') . '/u', $name);   // the typed letters start one of its words
2288:         // a state's two-letter code on its own ("pa"): the state, offered
2289:         if (mb_strlen($typed) === 2 && ($sc = array_search(strtoupper($typed), self::STATES, true)) !== false) {
2290:             $places[] = ['label' => ucwords((string) $sc), 'means' => in_array(strtoupper($typed), NpiBulk::STATES, true) ? 'a state to explore' : 'a state', 'q' => $sc . ' state', 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => true];
2291:             return array_map(fn ($x) => array_diff_key($x, ['ours' => 1]), $places);   // two letters: the state, nothing more
2292:         }
2293:         // a state's code before other words ("AZ a", "nm hik"): the state, offered (never assumed)
2294:         if (count($tk) === 2 && mb_strlen($tk[0]) === 2 && ($sc2 = array_search(strtoupper($tk[0]), self::STATES, true)) !== false) {
2295:             $places[] = ['label' => ucwords((string) $sc2), 'means' => in_array(strtoupper($tk[0]), NpiBulk::STATES, true) ? 'a state to explore' : 'a state', 'q' => $sc2 . ' state', 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => true];
2296:         }
2297:         // a kind of place, in OpenStreetMap's own words ("dog park", "playground", "water park"): the obvious one first
2298:         $kinds = [];
2299:         if (is_file(__DIR__ . '/OpenPlaces.php')) {
2300:             require_once __DIR__ . '/OpenPlaces.php';
2301:             foreach (OpenPlaces::tagWords() as $tw => $kk) {
2302:                 if (!str_starts_with($tw, $typed) || mb_strlen($typed) < 3 || count($kinds) >= 2) continue;
2303:                 $label = ucfirst($tw) . (str_ends_with($tw, 's') ? '' : 's');
2304:                 $kinds[] = ['label' => $label, 'means' => mb_strtolower((string) OpenPlaces::KINDS[$kk][0]) . ', nearest first', 'q' => $tw, 'kind' => 'kindof'];
2305:             }
2306:         }
2307:         // countries and states, and the peoples whose word is being typed ("mexi": Mexico, New Mexico; Mexican)
2308:         foreach ((array) $dem['places'] as $pl => $ws) {
2309:             if (!$starts((string) $pl)) continue;
2310:             $isState = isset(self::STATES[$pl]);
2311:             $places[] = ['label' => ucwords((string) $pl), 'means' => $isState ? (in_array(self::STATES[$pl], NpiBulk::STATES, true) ? 'a state to explore' : 'a state') : 'a country', 'q' => $isState ? $pl . ' state' : (string) $pl, 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => $isState && in_array(self::STATES[$pl], NpiBulk::STATES, true)];
2312:             $seen[(string) $pl] = 1;
2313:         }
2314:         // a country's people (a U.S. state's people read oddly as a cuisine: "Arizonan food"), and its country with it ("iri": Ireland)
2315:         foreach ((array) $dem['peoples'] as $pw => $pls) {
2316:             $pls = array_values(array_filter((array) $pls, fn ($x) => !isset(self::STATES[mb_strtolower((string) $x)])));
2317:             if (!$pls || !$starts((string) $pw) || str_ends_with((string) $pw, 's') || count($peoples) >= 2) continue;
2318:             $peoples[(string) $pw] = $pls;
2319:             foreach ($pls as $cn) if (!isset($seen[$k = mb_strtolower((string) $cn)])) { $seen[$k] = 1; $places[] = ['label' => (string) $cn, 'means' => 'a country', 'q' => $k, 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => false]; }
2320:         }
2321:         // tribal nations by name: their public side (the nation, its visitor information), decisions/0066
2322:         if (class_exists('Nations') || is_file(__DIR__ . '/Nations.php')) {
2323:             require_once __DIR__ . '/Nations.php';
2324:             try {
2325:                 foreach (Nations::all($pdo) as $n) {
2326:                     $nm = mb_strtolower((string) $n['name']);
2327:                     if (!$starts($nm) || isset($seen[$nm])) continue;
2328:                     $seen[$nm] = 1;
2329:                     $places[] = ['label' => (string) $n['name'], 'means' => 'a tribal nation: its land and visitor information', 'q' => $nm, 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => true];
2330:                     if (preg_match('/(^| )(' . preg_quote($typed, '/') . '\p{L}*)/u', $nm, $m) && count($peoples) < 2) $peoples[$m[2]] = $peoples[$m[2]] ?? [(string) $n['name']];
2331:                 }
2332:             } catch (Throwable) {}
2333:         }
2334:         // the U.S. town and county it names, the one near the person first
2335:         foreach (array_unique(array_merge([$typed], array_map(fn ($d) => mb_strtolower(trim((string) $d)), $done))) as $w) {
2336:             if (!$starts($w) || mb_strlen($w) < 4 || isset($seen[$w])) continue;
2337:             try {
2338:                 // a town outside our areas whose name starts a name we hold, typed out ("four corners": Four Corners Monument
2339:                 // over Four Corners, WY, decisions/0058 §25): the town stays among the places below, not ahead of the name
2340:                 $p0 = self::placeNamed($pdo, $w, null, true, true);
2341:                 if ($p0 && empty($p0['url']) && str_contains($w, ' ') && self::namesSomething($pdo, $w)) $p0 = null;
2342:                 if ($p0 && !isset($seen[$k = mb_strtolower((string) $p0['label'])])) {
2343:                     $seen[$k] = 1; $st = (string) ($p0['state'] ?? '');
2344:                     $places[] = ['label' => (string) $p0['label'], 'means' => 'a town' . ($st !== '' && isset($abbr[$st]) ? ' in ' . ucwords((string) $abbr[$st]) : ''), 'q' => mb_strtolower((string) $p0['label']), 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => !empty($p0['url'])];
2345:                 }
2346:                 $cs = $pdo->prepare('SELECT TRIM(county) AS county, TRIM(state) AS state FROM zip_coordinates WHERE county LIKE ? GROUP BY TRIM(county), TRIM(state) ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 2');
2347:                 $cs->execute([SearchText::esc($w) . '%']);
2348:                 foreach ($cs->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) {
2349:                     $cl = ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $c['county'])) . ' County, ' . $c['state'];
2350:                     if (isset($seen[$k = mb_strtolower($cl)])) continue; $seen[$k] = 1;
2351:                     $places[] = ['label' => $cl, 'means' => 'a county', 'q' => mb_strtolower($cl), 'kind' => 'place', 'ours' => in_array((string) $c['state'], NpiBulk::STATES, true)];
2352:                 }
2353:             } catch (Throwable) {}
2354:         }
2355:         // ours first among places (a state we cover, a nation, our towns), then the rest as found
2356:         usort($places, fn ($a, $b) => (empty($b['ours']) ? 0 : 1) <=> (empty($a['ours']) ? 0 : 1));
2357:         // a people brings its food and its culture
2358:         $more = [];
2359:         foreach ($peoples as $pw => $pls) {
2360:             $more[] = ['label' => ucwords($pw) . ' food', 'means' => 'restaurants and ' . ucwords($pw) . ' cuisine', 'q' => $pw . ' food', 'kind' => 'food'];
2361:             $more[] = ['label' => ucwords($pw) . ' culture', 'means' => 'heritage, history, museums, cultural centers', 'q' => $pw . ' culture', 'kind' => 'culture'];
2362:         }
2363:         return array_map(fn ($x) => array_diff_key($x, ['ours' => 1]), array_slice(array_merge($kinds, array_slice($places, 0, 4), $more), 0, 7));
2364:     }
2365: 
2366:     public static function suggest(PDO $pdo, string $q, array $ctx = []): array
2367:     {
2368:         $q = SearchText::spell(trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', mb_strtolower($q))));   // "theatre" reads as "theater" (names match both)
2369:         $out = ['items' => [], 'here' => false, 'place' => null];
2370:         if (mb_strlen($q) < 3) return $out;   // suggestions wait for three letters (Jason, 2026-11-11)
2371:         // "near me" and its kin say where, not what (decisions/0067, 2026-10-08): searched without them, so "me" is never
2372:         // Maine or "medical"; the words before them are finished. One still being typed ("near m") is offered whole.
2373:         self::$near = self::nearFrom($pdo, $ctx);
2374:         $q0 = $q;
2375:         $nm = self::nearMe($q);
2376:         if ($nm['near'] || $nm['partial'] !== '') {
2377:             $q = $nm['text']; $ctx['end'] = true;
2378:             $out['near_me'] = $nm['near'];
2379:             if ($nm['partial'] !== '') foreach (self::NEAR_ME_SAY as $say) if (str_starts_with($say, $nm['partial'])) {
2380:                 $out['near_complete'] = ['q' => trim($q . ' ' . $say), 'typed' => $q0, 'rest' => mb_substr(trim($q . ' ' . $say), mb_strlen($q0))];
2381:                 break;
2382:             }
2383:             if (mb_strlen($q) < 2) { if (!empty($out['near_complete'])) $out['complete'] = [$out['near_complete']]; unset($out['near_complete']); return $out; }
2384:         }
2385:         $place = self::placeFromScope($pdo, $ctx);
2386:         // no place set, but the words name one ("moving to seattle washington"): that's the place while typing too, as
2387:         // it is after Enter, so a situation answers there instead of asking where
2388:         // a place named in the words is the place for these words, set place or not (decisions/0066): "seattle
2389:         // washington" with St. Johns set answers for Seattle; the set place doesn't change
2390:         $onlyPlace = null;
2391:         // a last word of two letters still being typed that's also a state's code ("dog pa": park, or Pennsylvania?) isn't
2392:         // assumed to be the state: the words are read without it, and "in Pennsylvania" is offered (Jason, 2026-11-11: "if an
2393:         // abbreviation is possible maybe ask if they are looking for the state? Or in a state?")
2394:         $askState = null;
2395:         if (empty($ctx['end']) && preg_match('/^(.+)\s([a-z]{2})$/u', $q, $sm2) && !str_contains($q, ',') && ($sn2 = array_search(strtoupper($sm2[2]), self::STATES, true)) !== false) {
2396:             $askState = ['words' => trim($sm2[1]), 'state' => (string) $sn2];
2397:         }
2398:         if (mb_strlen($q) >= 4) { try { [$wp, $wrest] = self::findPlace($pdo, $askState ? $askState['words'] : $q); if ($wp && empty($wp['landmark'])) { $place = $wp; if (trim((string) $wrest) === '') $onlyPlace = $wp; } } catch (Throwable) {} }
2399:         $out['place'] = $place['label'] ?? null;
2400:         $items = [];
2401:         // "help" on its own could be either: ask (decisions/0060). "Legal help" isn't asked: it's local.
2402:         if (mb_strlen($q) >= 3 && ($ask = self::askHelp($q, $place, true))) $out['ask'] = $ask;
2403:         elseif ($onlyPlace && ($pc = self::placeCard($pdo, $onlyPlace, $q))) $out['ask'] = $pc;   // only a place: what about it?
2404:         $tools = mb_strlen($q) >= 4 ? self::tools($q, $place, true) : [];   // the site's own tools (the Trip Planner)
2405:         if (!class_exists('SearchIndex') || !SearchIndex::ready($pdo)) { $out['items'] = $items; return $out; }
2406:         $esc = SearchText::esc($q);
2407:         $c = $place ? self::centerOf($pdo, $place) : null;
2408:         // the last word may still be being typed (no space after it yet): what the words most likely are once it's
2409:         // finished ("live mu" is live music, "self stor" self storage), from the words that lead somewhere, the commonest
2410:         // first (decisions/0067, 2026-10-07). Read for what they mean, names and what Enter would find.
2411:         $fin = [];

2413:         $fq = $fin[0] ?? $q;
2414:         // what the words mean (decisions/0067): "xrays" → Diagnostic Imaging Centers, "cardio" → gyms, "live mu" → Music &

2416:         // What people opened after the same words leads. The sure ones order everything below (step 3: rank by meaning).

2421:             $phr = $rest0 !== '' ? [$rest0] : ($fin ?: [$q]);
2422:             try {

2424:                     if (isset($meantRows[$k0 = mb_strtolower($m['label'])])) continue;
2425:                     // the second way to finish it: only what it surely means, when the first means nothing for sure; never the third
2426:                     if ($pi > 0 && (!$m['sure'] || $sureL || $pi > 1)) continue;
2427:                     $meantRows[$k0] = $m + ['for' => $ph];
2428:                     if ($m['sure'] && $pi === 0) { $sureL[$k0] = 1; $sureIds[] = $m['ids']; }   // the likeliest words order the rest, kind by kind
2429:                     if (count($meantRows) >= 4) break 2;
2430:                 }
2431:             } catch (Throwable $e) { error_log('suggest meant: ' . $e->getMessage()); }
2432:         }
2433:         $nameRows = function (string $qq) use ($pdo, $place, $c): array {
2434:             $e = SearchText::esc($qq);
2435:             $p = [':a' => $e . '%', ':b' => '% ' . $e . '%'];
2436:             $wide = str_contains(trim($qq), ' ') && mb_strlen(trim($qq)) >= 8;

2438:             $alsoSql = '';
2439:             foreach (array_slice(SearchText::spellings($qq), 1) as $i => $alt) {

2442:                 $alsoSql .= ' OR si.label' . SearchText::CI . ' LIKE :sa' . $i . ' OR si.label' . SearchText::CI . ' LIKE :sb' . $i;

2446:                  . ($here !== '' ? ", COALESCE($here, 0) AS here" : ', 0 AS here')
2447:                  . " FROM search_index si WHERE si.kind IN ('listing', 'outdoors', 'story', 'group', 'experience') AND (si.label" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE :a OR si.label" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE :b" . $alsoSql . ")"

2456:         };

2850:                 $m0 = $past[0]['miles']; $to = null;
2851:                 foreach ([5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250] as $r0) if ($r0 >= $m0 && (!ctype_digit($reach) || $r0 > (int) $reach)) { $to = $r0; break; }
2852:                 if ($to) {
2853:                     $nIn = count(array_filter($past, fn ($h) => $h['miles'] <= $to));
2854:                     $widenRow[] = ['label' => $nIn === 1 ? $past[0]['label'] : $nIn . ' named “' . ($known !== '' ? $known : $q) . '”', 'crumb' => ($nIn === 1 ? $past[0]['what'] . ' · ' : 'Nearest ' ) . round($m0) . ' mi, past your ' . ($reach === 'place' ? 'place' : $reach . ' mi') . ' · Widen to ' . $to . ' mi',
2855:                                    'widen' => (string) $to, 'kind' => 'widen', 'here' => false];
2856:                 }
2857:             }
2858:         }
2859:         $leadT = array_column(array_filter($tools, fn ($t) => $t['lead']), 'item');
2860:         $afterT = array_column(array_filter($tools, fn ($t) => !$t['lead']), 'item');
2861:         // as many as match, up to 15, until Enter or a pick (Jason, 2026-10-07). While it's still one word ("for"), what
2862:         // people most likely mean leads: places and kinds of thing, then listings and outdoor places that start with
2863:         // it, and stories, groups and experiences (discover and community) after them
2864:         if (!str_contains($q, ' ')) {

2866:             $hitsT = array_values(array_filter(array_merge($local, $far), fn ($h) => !$isCom($h)));
2867:             usort($hitsT, fn ($a, $b) => [!$a['agree'], !$a['start'], !$a['here']] <=> [!$b['agree'], !$b['start'], !$b['here']]);
2868:             $com = array_values(array_filter(array_merge($local, $far), $isCom));
2869:             $out['items'] = array_slice(array_merge($leadT, $platform, $rest, $widenRow, $groups, $hitsT, $ahead, $com, $afterT), 0, 15);
2870:         } elseif ($sureL) {
2871:             // the words mean a kind of place for sure ("live music"): that kind, then its places, then what Enter finds; names
2872:             // that only share a word, and only whole ("music", not Musick), after the rest
2873:             $kindRows = array_values(array_filter($rest, fn ($x) => !empty($x['sure'])));
2874:             $rest = array_values(array_filter($rest, fn ($x) => empty($x['sure'])));
2875:             // no place set (Everywhere): everything is near enough
2876:             if (!$place) { $local = array_merge($local, $far); $far = []; }
2877:             $agreeL = array_values(array_filter($local, fn ($h) => $h['agree']));
2878:             $otherL = array_values(array_filter($local, fn ($h) => !$h['agree'] && $h['whole']));
2879:             $farS = array_values(array_filter($far, fn ($h) => $h['agree'] || $h['whole']));
2880:             // what Enter would find: of the kind meant, or with the words whole in the name
2881:             $lw = (string) preg_replace('/^.* /', '', $fq);
2882:             $agA = fn ($a) => isset($sureL[mb_strtolower(SearchText::catLabel(trim(explode(' · ', (string) preg_replace('/^.*› /', '', (string) $a['crumb']))[0])))]);
2883:             $ahead = array_values(array_filter($ahead, fn ($a) => $agA($a) || preg_match('/(^|[^\p{L}])' . preg_quote($lw, '/') . '([^\p{L}]|$)/iu', (string) $a['label'])));
2884:             usort($ahead, fn ($a, $b) => !$agA($a) <=> !$agA($b));   // of the kind meant first
2885:             usort($farS, fn ($a, $b) => [!$a['agree'], $a['rank'] ?? 0, $a['miles'] ?? 9999] <=> [!$b['agree'], $b['rank'] ?? 0, $b['miles'] ?? 9999]);
2886:             $out['items'] = array_slice(array_merge($leadT, $platform, $kindRows, $widenRow, $groups, array_slice($agreeL, 0, 6), $ahead, $rest, array_slice($otherL, 0, 3), $farS, $afterT), 0, 15);
2887:         } else $out['items'] = array_slice(array_merge($leadT, $platform, $widenRow, $groups, array_slice($local, 0, 6), $ahead, $rest, $far, $afterT), 0, 15);
2888:         // topics our guides know about (decisions/0133), right after the tools the words ask for
2889:         if (mb_strlen($q) >= 3 && is_file(__DIR__ . '/Topics.php')) {
2890:             try {
2891:                 require_once __DIR__ . '/Topics.php';

2894:                 if ($kn) { array_splice($out['items'], count($leadT), 0, $kn); $out['items'] = array_slice($out['items'], 0, 15); }
2895:             } catch (Throwable $e) { error_log('suggest topics: ' . $e->getMessage()); }
2896:         }

2898:         // locations as someone types"): towns, counties and states that start with the words, and "pizza in Phoenix, AZ"
2899:         // for words ending in a town being typed, right after the site's own tools, up to five; the rest keep their order
2900:         $pl = array_values(array_filter($out['items'], fn ($x) => ($x['kind'] ?? '') === 'place'));
2901:         if ($pl) {
2902:             $pl = array_slice($pl, 0, 5);
2903:             $restI = array_values(array_filter($out['items'], fn ($x) => ($x['kind'] ?? '') !== 'place'));
2904:             $nLead = 0; foreach ($restI as $x) { if (($x['kind'] ?? '') === 'platform' || in_array($x, $leadT, true)) $nLead++; else break; }
2905:             $out['items'] = array_slice(array_merge(array_slice($restI, 0, $nLead), $pl, array_slice($restI, $nLead)), 0, 15);
2906:         }
2907:         // a tool the words ask for: no "planning open now"
2908:         if ($leadT && !empty($out['complete'])) $out['complete'] = array_values(array_filter($out['complete'], fn ($c) => !preg_match('/ (open now|open this weekend)$/', (string) $c['q'])));
2909:         $out['here'] = (bool) array_filter($hits, fn ($h) => $h['here']);
2910:         // no place set, and the words are for somewhere to go: "near me", "local", a kind of place they surely mean, a thing
2911:         // to do (decisions/0067, 2026-10-08). The panel asks for a place. A situation's preview asks on its own.
2912:         $out['need_place'] = !$place && !$onlyPlace && empty($out['preview']['need_place']) && empty($out['preview'])
2913:             && !array_filter($hits, fn ($h) => str_starts_with(mb_strtolower((string) $h['label']), $q) && str_contains($q, ' '))   // a business's name being typed
2914:             && (!empty($out['near_me']) || self::saysHere($q0) || $sureL || array_filter($out['items'], fn ($x) => in_array($x['kind'] ?? '', ['category', 'tag'], true)));
2915:         // once each: a kind can come from its name and from what the words mean ("yoga class": Yoga Instruction)
2916:         $seenR = [];

''); if (isset($seenR[$k]) || isset($seenR[mb_strtolower((string) $x['label'])])) return false; $seenR[$k] = 1; if (($x['kind'] ?? '') === 'category') $seenR[mb_strtolower((string) $x['label'])] = 1; return true; }));
2918:         // the most likely first (Jason, 2026-11-11: "a whole word vs partial word match for scoring … 3 letters of 8 vs 8 of
2919:         // 8"): each suggestion scored by how its name matches what was typed, the order above kept among equals
2920:         // the words name a thing (a sea lion): the ways to it stand in for the loose guesses at a kind of business ("Fishing Lakes &
2921:         // Ponds" for sea lions); a kind the words surely mean stays

2923:         // an outing being typed: kinds read from the words that only frame it ("take": Takeout) aren't what's meant; and the
2924:         // kind of business offered with the full words isn't offered again below

2926:         if ($bizIntent && array_filter((array) ($out['intents'] ?? []), fn ($x) => $x === $bizIntent)) $out['items'] = array_values(array_filter($out['items'], fn ($x) => !(($x['kind'] ?? '') === 'category' && mb_strtolower((string) $x['label']) === mb_strtolower($bizIntent['label']))));

2928:         // a name typed out (two words or more) that's a town outside our areas and also starts a name we hold ("four corners"):
2929:         // the name we hold leads, the town right after it (decisions/0058 §25; CLAUDE.md, Places in search)
2930:         if (str_contains($q, ' ')) {

2936:             }

2950:         return $out;
2951:     }
2952: 
2953:     /** What the typed words may carry on to: situation phrases first, then the 5 W's around a thing ("parks" -> "parks for kids"). */
2954:     private static function completions(PDO $pdo, string $q, ?array $place): array
2955:     {
2956:         $t = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', mb_strtolower($q)));
2957:         if (mb_strlen($t) < 3) return [];
2958:         $out = class_exists('Situations') ? Situations::complete($pdo, $t, 4) : [];
2959:         // the words already name a situation (by its phrase or by what they mean): only its own completions, never
2960:         // "bathroom open now" or "bite by something to eat"
2961:         $named = class_exists('Situations') && Situations::read($pdo, $t);
2962:         // the Why phrases carry words on too ("landmarks i should" -> "...check out", "...see", "...visit")
2963:         if (!$out && !$named) {
2964:             $ws = explode(' ', $t);
2965:             for ($k = min(3, count($ws)); $k >= 1 && !$out; $k--) {
2966:                 $tail = implode(' ', array_slice($ws, -$k));
2967:                 if ($k === 1 && (mb_strlen($tail) < 4 || count($ws) >= 3)) continue;   // one word of a longer search carries on to anything
2968:                 foreach (array_keys(self::WHY_PHRASES) as $ph) {
2969:                     if (!str_starts_with($ph, $tail) || $ph === $tail || !str_contains($ph, ' ')) continue;
2970:                     $rest = mb_substr($ph, mb_strlen($tail));
2971:                     if (!str_starts_with($rest, ' ')) continue;   // whole words only
2972:                     $out[] = ['q' => $t . $rest, 'typed' => $t, 'rest' => $rest];
2973:                     if (count($out) >= 4) break;
2974:                 }
2975:             }
2976:         }
2977:         // a thing named (on its own or with a where): when, who and where it could carry on with. Only when no situation
2978:         // carries the words on ("car" is Car broke down, not "car open now")
2979:         if (!$out && !$named) {
2980:             $core = trim(preg_replace('/ (near|in|around|at) .*$/', '', ' ' . $t));
2981:             $core = trim(implode(' ', array_filter(explode(' ', $core), fn ($w) => !in_array($w, self::DESCRIBE, true))));
2982:             $isTag = false;
2983:             foreach (self::TAGS as $tag => $g) if (self::root(mb_strtolower($tag)) === self::root($core)) { $isTag = true; break; }
2984:             $thing = $core !== '' && ($isTag || self::findCategory($pdo, $core) || (!str_contains($core, ' ') && isset(self::whatVocab($pdo)[self::root($core)])));
2985:             if ($thing) {
2986:                 $where = $place ? trim(explode('·', (string) $place['label'])[0]) : '';
2987:                 $ext = $isTag ? [' for kids', ' worth the drive', ' near ' . ($where ?: 'me')] : [' open now', ' open this weekend', ' near ' . ($where ?: 'me')];
2988:                 foreach ($ext as $x) {
2989:                     $key = trim(explode(' ', trim($x))[0]);
2990:                     if (preg_match('/\b' . preg_quote($key, '/') . '\b/', $t)) continue;   // already said ("parks near me" isn't offered "near" again)
2991:                     $out[] = ['q' => $t . $x, 'typed' => $t, 'rest' => $x];
2992:                 }
2993:             }
2994:         }
2995:         // (a pattern in what's searched is offered apart, as "Maybe try": maybeTry(), decisions/0093)
2996:         return array_slice($out, 0, 6);
2997:     }
2998: 
2999:     /**
3000:      * "Maybe try" (decisions/0093, Jason 2026-10-15): a pattern the search noticed, offered as its own suggestion, never
3001:      * as "what other people search". Only phrases searched by real visitors on at least 3 different days that found
3002:      * something, made only of words the search already understands (never a name, an address, anything personal), and
3003:      * not already among the predictions. At most 2.
3004:      */
3005:     private static function maybeTry(PDO $pdo, string $t, array $have): array
3006:     {
3007:         if (!is_file(__DIR__ . '/Learning.php')) return [];
3008:         require_once __DIR__ . '/Learning.php';
3009:         $out = [];
3010:         foreach (Learning::completions($pdo, $t, 4) as $c) {
3011:             if (in_array($c['q'], $have, true)) continue;
3012:             $out[] = ['q' => $c['q'], 'typed' => $t, 'rest' => $c['rest']];
3013:             if (count($out) >= 2) break;
3014:         }
3015:         return $out;
3016:     }

3018:     /** A situation's needs with no answers yet, and the ask for a place (decisions/0061). */
3019:     private static function askPlace(PDO $pdo, array $sits): ?array
3020:     {
3021:         $a = Situations::answer($pdo, $sits, null, ['per' => 0]);
3022:         if (!$a) return null;
3023:         foreach ($a['needs'] as $i => $n) { $a['needs'][$i]['items'] = []; $a['needs'][$i]['incomplete'] = 0; }
3024:         return $a + ['place' => null, 'need_place' => true];
3025:     }
3026: 
3027:     /** The situations a search names, with each need's solutions, or null (decisions/0061). */
3028:     private static function situationFor(PDO $pdo, string $q, ?array $place, ?array $area, string $reach, array $ctx, array $drop): ?array
3029:     {
3030:         if (in_array('situation', $drop, true) || !is_file(__DIR__ . '/Situations.php')) return null;
3031:         require_once __DIR__ . '/Situations.php';
3032:         try {
3033:             $sits = Situations::read($pdo, $q);
3034:             if (!$sits) return null;
3035:             // no place set: "nearest" has nothing to measure from, so ask where first (never a guess hundreds of miles off)
3036:             if (!$place || !empty($place['facts'])) {
3037:                 $ask = self::askPlace($pdo, $sits);
3038:                 // a state's code in the words ("ca tow truck"): asked, never assumed ("In California?"), and the question is
3039:                 // where in it (Jason, 2026-11-11: refine by asking more questions)
3040:                 if ($ask && preg_match('/(?:^|\s)([a-z]{2})(?:\s|$)/u', mb_strtolower(trim($q)), $sc) && ($sn = array_search(strtoupper($sc[1]), self::STATES, true)) !== false)
3041:                     $ask['state_hint'] = ['code' => strtoupper($sc[1]), 'name' => ucwords((string) $sn)];
3042:                 return $ask;
3043:             }
3044:             $c = $area ? [$area['lat'], $area['lon']] : (($cc = $place ? self::centerOf($pdo, $place) : null) ? [$cc['lat'], $cc['lon']] : null);
3045:             $a = Situations::answer($pdo, $sits, $place && empty($place['facts']) ? $place : null,
3046:                 ['center' => $c, 'reach' => $area ? (string) $area['radius'] : $reach, 'at' => is_array($ctx['at'] ?? null) ? $ctx['at'] : null]);
3047:             // nothing listed for any need there yet ("Seattle, WA", outside our areas): the situation still answers, saying so
3048:             // outside our regions: the town's middle (about a mile), so the panel can ask OpenStreetMap for businesses (decisions/0065)
3049:             $oc = !empty($place['outside']) && $c ? [round((float) $c[0], 2), round((float) $c[1], 2)] : null;
3050:             return $a ? $a + ['place' => $place['label'] ?? null, 'none_here' => !array_filter($a['needs'], fn ($n) => $n['items']), 'outside' => !empty($place['outside']), 'center' => $oc] : null;
3051:         } catch (Throwable $e) { error_log('situation: ' . $e->getMessage()); return null; }
3052:     }
3053: 
3054:     /** A place's middle: its first ZIP, else an area's or region's own center. ['lat', 'lon'] or null. */
3055:     public static function centerOf(PDO $pdo, array $place): ?array
3056:     {
3057:         if (isset($place['lat'], $place['lon'])) return ['lat' => (float) $place['lat'], 'lon' => (float) $place['lon']];
3058:         $zip = (string) ($place['zips'][0] ?? '');
3059:         if ($zip !== '') {
3060:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE zip = ? LIMIT 1');
3061:             $st->execute([$zip]);
3062:             if ($z = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) return ['lat' => (float) $z['latitude'], 'lon' => (float) $z['longitude']];
3063:         }
3064:         $t = ['geo_hub' => 'geo_hubs', 'hub' => 'hubs'][$place['level'] ?? ''] ?? null;
3065:         if ($t && !empty($place['slug'])) {
3066:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT center_lat, center_lon FROM $t WHERE slug = ? LIMIT 1");
3067:             $st->execute([(string) $place['slug']]);
3068:             if (($c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) && $c['center_lat'] !== null) return ['lat' => (float) $c['center_lat'], 'lon' => (float) $c['center_lon']];
3069:         }
3070:         return null;
3071:     }
3072: 
3073:     private static function elsewhere(PDO $pdo, array $place, string $what): ?array
3074:     {
3075:         // the words that name the thing: not "new", "good", "place" (decisions/0058 §36)
3076:         $stop = array_merge(['in', 'near', 'the', 'a', 'an', 'and', 'of', 'for', 'to', 'at', 'on', 'with', 'places', 'place'], self::DESCRIBE, SearchIndex::STOP, SearchIndex::SOFT);
3077:         $words = array_slice(array_values(array_filter(preg_split('/\s+/u', mb_strtolower($what)) ?: [], fn ($w) => mb_strlen($w) >= 3 && !in_array($w, $stop, true))), 0, 4);
3078:         if (!$words) return null;
3079:         $center = null;
3080:         // a place outside our areas ("Seattle, WA"): only towns within about 150 miles of it, and none when we don't know
3081:         // where it is (never Arizona's towns for Seattle); the results then carry on on Google Maps
3082:         if (!empty($place['outside'])) {
3083:             if (!isset($place['lat'], $place['lon'])) return null;
3084:             $center = ['lat' => (float) $place['lat'], 'lon' => (float) $place['lon'], 'radius' => 150];
3085:         }
3086:         $zip = $center ? '' : (string) ($place['zips'][0] ?? '');
3087:         if ($zip !== '') {
3088:             $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT latitude, longitude FROM zip_coordinates WHERE zip = ? LIMIT 1');
3089:             $st->execute([$zip]);
3090:             if ($z = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) $center = ['lat' => (float) $z['latitude'], 'lon' => (float) $z['longitude'], 'radius' => 200];
3091:         }
3092:         if (!$center && !empty($place['level']) && !empty($place['slug'])) {   // an area or region: its own middle
3093:             $t = ['geo_hub' => 'geo_hubs', 'hub' => 'hubs'][$place['level']] ?? null;
3094:             if ($t) {
3095:                 $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT center_lat, center_lon FROM $t WHERE slug = ? LIMIT 1");
3096:                 $st->execute([(string) $place['slug']]);
3097:                 if (($c = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC)) && $c['center_lat'] !== null) $center = ['lat' => (float) $c['center_lat'], 'lon' => (float) $c['center_lon'], 'radius' => 300];
3098:             }
3099:         }
3100:         // each word, singular or plural ("hat" finds Hats Off)
3101:         $alts = [];
3102:         foreach ($words as $w) $alts[$w] = array_values(array_unique([$w, preg_match('/s$/', $w) ? substr($w, 0, -1) : $w . 's']));
3103:         $rows = SearchIndex::rows($pdo, null, $center, ['kinds' => ['listing'], 'words' => array_merge(...array_values($alts)), 'geo' => true, 'limit' => 400, 'not_place' => $place]);
3104:         // every word, not any one ("new hat" is hats, not New Covenant Church)
3105:         $rows = array_values(array_filter($rows, function ($r) use ($alts) {
3106:             $t = SearchIndex::norm((string) ($r['terms'] ?? ''), (string) $r['label']);
3107:             foreach ($alts as $forms) {
3108:                 $ok = false;
3109:                 foreach ($forms as $f) { $n = trim(SearchIndex::norm($f)); if (str_contains($t, ' ' . $n . (mb_strlen($n) <= 3 ? ' ' : ''))) { $ok = true; break; } }
3110:                 if (!$ok) return false;
3111:             }
3112:             return true;
3113:         }));
3114:         // each one belongs to the nearest town area we cover (within 30 mi), else its own town
3115:         $areas = [];
3116:         try {
3117:             foreach ($pdo->query('SELECT mc.slug, mc.name, COALESCE(mc.center_lat, z.latitude) AS lat, COALESCE(mc.center_lon, z.longitude) AS lon
3118:                                     FROM micro_clusters mc LEFT JOIN zip_coordinates z ON z.zip = mc.primary_zip')->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $c) {
3119:                 // the place's own area isn't "elsewhere", unless the place is one town in it (the rest of the area is)
3120:                 if ($c['lat'] !== null && (string) $c['slug'] !== '' && ((string) $c['slug'] !== (string) ($place['slug'] ?? '') || !empty($place['town']))) $areas[] = $c;
3121:             }
3122:         } catch (Throwable) {}
3123:         $zipTown = [];
3124:         $areaName = function (array $c) use ($pdo, &$zipTown): string {   // an area still named "ZIP 85939" reads as its town
3125:             $n = PlaceScope::publicName((string) $c['name']);
3126:             if (!preg_match('/^ZIP (\d{5})/', $n, $zm)) return $n;
3127:             if (!array_key_exists($zm[1], $zipTown)) {
3128:                 $st = $pdo->prepare('SELECT city, state FROM zip_coordinates WHERE zip = ? LIMIT 1'); $st->execute([$zm[1]]);
3129:                 $z = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
3130:                 $zipTown[$zm[1]] = $z ? ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $z['city'])) . ', ' . $z['state'] : $n;
3131:             }
3132:             return $zipTown[$zm[1]];
3133:         };
3134:         $mi = fn ($a, $b, $c, $d) => 3958.8 * 2 * asin(min(1, sqrt(sin(deg2rad($c - $a) / 2) ** 2 + cos(deg2rad($a)) * cos(deg2rad($c)) * sin(deg2rad($d - $b) / 2) ** 2)));
3135:         foreach ($rows as &$r) {
3136:             $best = null; $bd = 30.0;
3137:             foreach ($areas as $c) { $d = $mi((float) $r['lat'], (float) $r['lon'], (float) $c['lat'], (float) $c['lon']); if ($d < $bd) { $bd = $d; $best = $c; } }
3138:             $r['area'] = $best ? $areaName($best) : trim(implode(', ', array_filter([ucwords(mb_strtolower((string) $r['city'])), (string) $r['state']])));
3139:             $r['area_slug'] = $best ? (string) $best['slug'] : '';
3140:         }
3141:         unset($r);
3142:         $towns = [];
3143:         foreach ($rows as $r) {
3144:             $town = (string) $r['area'];
3145:             if ($town === '') continue;
3146:             $t = &$towns[$town];
3147:             $t ??= ['town' => $town, 'slug' => (string) $r['area_slug'], 'miles' => null, 'count' => 0, 'items' => []];
3148:             $t['count']++;
3149:             $mi = isset($r['miles']) ? round((float) $r['miles']) : null;
3150:             if ($mi !== null && ($t['miles'] === null || $mi < $t['miles'])) $t['miles'] = $mi;
3151:             if (count($t['items']) < 3) $t['items'][] = ['id' => (int) $r['item_id'], 'label' => (string) $r['label'], 'sub' => (string) $r['sub'], 'lat' => (float) $r['lat'], 'lon' => (float) $r['lon'], 'miles' => $mi];
3152:             unset($t);
3153:         }
3154:         if (!$towns) return !empty($place['outside']) ? null : ['place' => (string) $place['label'], 'what' => $what, 'towns' => []];   // outside our areas: the results say it, with Google Maps
3155:         $towns = array_values($towns);
3156:         usort($towns, fn ($a, $b) => [$a['miles'] ?? 9999, -$a['count']] <=> [$b['miles'] ?? 9999, -$b['count']]);
3157:         return ['place' => (string) $place['label'], 'what' => $what, 'towns' => array_slice($towns, 0, 5)];
3158:     }

3160:     /** Up to 5 matching listings and the total, in the place (or town) asked about. */
3161:     private static function listings(PDO $pdo, string $text, ?array $place, ?array $cat, array $conds, ?array $area = null): array
3162:     {
3163:         $in = "'" . implode("','", self::STATUSES) . "'";
3164:         $params = [];
3165:         $where = [];
3166:         if ($area) {
3167:             // within the distance: a box first, then the exact distance
3168:             $dLat = $area['radius'] / 69.0; $dLon = $area['radius'] / max(0.0001, 69.0 * cos(deg2rad($area['lat'])));
3169:             $where[] = 'e.latitude BETWEEN :la1 AND :la2 AND e.longitude BETWEEN :lo1 AND :lo2';
3170:             $where[] = '(3958.8 * 2 * ASIN(SQRT(POWER(SIN(RADIANS(e.latitude - :lat_a) / 2), 2) + COS(RADIANS(:lat_b)) * COS(RADIANS(e.latitude)) * POWER(SIN(RADIANS(e.longitude - :lon_a) / 2), 2)))) <= :rad';
3171:             $params += [':la1' => $area['lat'] - $dLat, ':la2' => $area['lat'] + $dLat, ':lo1' => $area['lon'] - $dLon, ':lo2' => $area['lon'] + $dLon,
3172:                         ':lat_a' => $area['lat'], ':lat_b' => $area['lat'], ':lon_a' => $area['lon'], ':rad' => $area['radius']];
3173:         } elseif ($place && ($place['level'] ?? '') !== '') {
3174:             $param = ['cluster' => 'cluster', 'geo_hub' => 'geo', 'hub' => 'hub'][$place['level']];
3175:             $s = PlaceScope::resolve($pdo, [$param => (string) $place['slug']]);
3176:             if ($s && $s['found']) $where[] = $s['sql'];
3177:         } elseif ($place && !empty($place['city'])) {
3178:             $where[] = "JSON_UNQUOTE(m_city.meta_value)" . SearchText::CI . " = :city";
3179:             $params[':city'] = $place['city'];
3180:         } elseif ($place && !empty($place['state'])) {
3181:             $where[] = "JSON_UNQUOTE(m_state.meta_value)" . SearchText::CI . " = :st";
3182:             $params[':st'] = $place['state'];
3183:         }
3184:         $catWords = $cat ? trim((string) ($cat['words'] ?? '')) : '';
3185:         if ($cat && $catWords === '') { $where[] = 'e.category_id IN (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id = :cat_a OR parent_id = :cat_b' . CategoryAlso::sqlFor($pdo, (int) $cat['id']) . ')'; $params += [':cat_a' => $cat['id'], ':cat_b' => $cat['id']]; }
3186:         if (in_array('phone', $conds, true)) $where[] = "COALESCE(JSON_UNQUOTE(m_phone.meta_value), '') <> ''";
3187:         if (in_array('website', $conds, true)) $where[] = "COALESCE(JSON_UNQUOTE(m_web.meta_value), '') <> ''";
3188:         if ($text === '' && !$cat && !$place) return [[], 0];
3189:         $inner = "SELECT e.id, e.name AS business_name, e.category_id, COALESCE(c.name, CAST(JSON_UNQUOTE(m_cat.meta_value) AS CHAR(120))) COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci AS category,
3190:                          g.name AS category_group, " . ($catWords !== '' ? '(CASE WHEN e.category_id IN (SELECT id FROM categories WHERE id = ' . (int) $cat['id'] . ' OR parent_id = ' . (int) $cat['id'] . ') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)' : '0') . " AS in_cat,
3191:                          CAST(JSON_UNQUOTE(m_city.meta_value) AS CHAR(120)) AS city, CAST(JSON_UNQUOTE(m_state.meta_value) AS CHAR(40)) AS state,
3192:                          JSON_UNQUOTE(m_desc.meta_value) AS description,
3193:                          REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(m_offer.meta_value AS CHAR(2000)), '[', ' '), ']', ' '), '\"', ' ') COLLATE utf8mb4_general_ci AS offerings
3194:                     FROM entities e
3195:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_city  ON m_city.entity_id  = e.id AND m_city.meta_key  = 'city'
3196:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_state ON m_state.entity_id = e.id AND m_state.meta_key = 'state'
3197:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_cat   ON m_cat.entity_id   = e.id AND m_cat.meta_key   = 'category'
3198:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_desc  ON m_desc.entity_id  = e.id AND m_desc.meta_key  = 'description'
3199:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_offer ON m_offer.entity_id = e.id AND m_offer.meta_key = 'offerings'
3200:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_phone ON m_phone.entity_id = e.id AND m_phone.meta_key = 'phone'
3201:                     LEFT JOIN entity_metadata m_web   ON m_web.entity_id   = e.id AND m_web.meta_key   = 'website'
3202:                     LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = e.category_id
3203:                     LEFT JOIN categories g ON g.id = c.parent_id
3204:                    WHERE e.entity_type = 'merchant' AND e.is_suppressed = 0 AND e.status IN ($in)" . ($where ? ' AND ' . implode(' AND ', $where) : '');
3205:         $run = function (bool $strict) use ($pdo, $inner, $params, $text, $catWords): array {
3206:             $qp = [];
3207:             $qs = $text !== '' ? SearchText::where($text, $strict, $qp, ['x.offerings']) : '';
3208:             if ($text !== '' && $qs === '') $qs = '1 = 0';
3209:             if ($catWords !== '') {   // a category read from words: in the category, or the words in its name, offerings or description
3210:                 $cp = [];
3211:                 $cs = SearchText::where($catWords, $strict, $cp, ['x.offerings']);
3212:                 $cs = str_replace(':t', ':ct', $cs);
3213:                 foreach ($cp as $k => $v) $qp[str_replace(':t', ':ct', $k)] = $v;
3214:                 $cs = '(x.in_cat = 1' . ($cs !== '' ? ' OR ' . $cs : '') . ')';
3215:                 $qs = $qs !== '' ? "$qs AND $cs" : $cs;
3216:             }
3217:             $w = $qs !== '' ? " WHERE $qs" : '';
3218:             $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT x.id, x.business_name, x.category_id, x.category, x.city, x.state FROM ($inner) x$w ORDER BY (x.business_name" . SearchText::CI . " LIKE :pre) DESC, x.business_name LIMIT 5");
3219:             $st->execute($params + $qp + [':pre' => SearchText::esc($text) . '%']);
3220:             $rows = $st->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
3221:             // fewer than 5 is all of them: counted without reading the listings a second time (2026-10-14: speed)
3222:             if (count($rows) < 5) return [$rows, count($rows)];
3223:             $n = $pdo->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ($inner) x$w");
3224:             $n->execute($params + $qp);
3225:             return [$rows, (int) $n->fetchColumn()];
3226:         };
3227:         [$rows, $total] = $run(true);
3228:         if ($text !== '' && $total < SearchText::LOOSE_BELOW) { [$r2, $t2] = $run(false); if ($t2 > $total) [$rows, $total] = [$r2, $t2]; }
3229:         $out = array_map(fn ($r) => ['id' => (int) $r['id'], 'label' => (string) $r['business_name'],
3230:             'sub' => trim(implode(' · ', array_filter([(string) $r['category'], trim(implode(', ', array_filter([(string) $r['city'], (string) $r['state']])))]))),
3231:             'url' => '/listing/view.php?id=' . (int) $r['id'], 'peek' => true], $rows);
3232:         return [$out, $total];
3233:     }

3244:         } catch (Throwable $e) { error_log('experiencesFor: ' . $e->getMessage()); return []; }

3248:     private static function journeysFor(PDO $pdo, string $text, ?array $place): array

3268:     {

3297:     /** The peek card: public details only; a confidential location shows the phone only (decisions/0044). */
3298:     public static function peek(PDO $pdo, int $id): ?array
3299:     {
3300:         $in = "'" . implode("','", self::STATUSES) . "'";
3301:         $st = $pdo->prepare("SELECT e.id, e.name, e.status, e.category_id, e.latitude, e.longitude, c.name AS category, c.slug AS category_slug FROM entities e LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = e.category_id
3302:                               WHERE e.id = ? AND e.entity_type = 'merchant' AND e.is_suppressed = 0 AND e.status IN ($in)");
3303:         $st->execute([$id]);
3304:         $e = $st->fetch(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);
3305:         if (!$e) return null;
3306:         $m = [];
3307:         $ms = $pdo->prepare("SELECT meta_key, meta_value FROM entity_metadata WHERE entity_id = ? AND meta_key IN ('address','city','state','phone','website','hours','description','contact_url')");
3308:         $ms->execute([$id]);
3309:         foreach ($ms->fetchAll(PDO::FETCH_ASSOC) as $r) $m[$r['meta_key']] = json_decode((string) $r['meta_value'], true) ?? $r['meta_value'];
3310:         $private = ListingSafety::isConfidential($e['category_id'] !== null ? (int) $e['category_id'] : null, (string) $e['name']);
3311:         $town = trim(implode(', ', array_filter([(string) ($m['city'] ?? ''), (string) ($m['state'] ?? '')])));
3312:         require_once __DIR__ . '/NameText.php';
3313:         $town = NameText::full($town);
3314:         return ['id' => (int) $e['id'], 'name' => NameText::listing((string) $e['name'], (string) $e['status']), 'category' => (string) ($e['category'] ?? ''), 'category_slug' => (string) ($e['category_slug'] ?? ''),
3315:                 'address' => $private ? null : trim(implode(', ', array_filter([(string) ($m['address'] ?? ''), $town]))), 'town' => $town,
3316:                 'private_location' => $private, 'mailing_only' => !$private && (require_once __DIR__ . '/ListingActions.php') && ListingActions::isMailbox((string) ($m['address'] ?? '')),
3317:                 'approx' => !$private && ((require_once __DIR__ . '/ListingActions.php') && ListingActions::isMailbox((string) ($m['address'] ?? '')) || self::approx($pdo, (int) $e['id'])),
3318:                 'phone' => (string) ($m['phone'] ?? ''), 'website' => $private ? '' : (string) ($m['website'] ?? ''),
3319:                 'contact_url' => (string) ($m['contact_url'] ?? ''), 'hours' => is_array($m['hours'] ?? null) ? array_slice($m['hours'], 0, 7) : [],
3320:                 'about' => mb_strimwidth((string) ($m['description'] ?? ''), 0, 240, '…'),
3321:                 'guide' => (require_once __DIR__ . '/Guides.php') ? Guides::lineAbout($pdo, 'entity:' . (int) $e['id'], (string) $e['name']) : null,
3322:                 'suggest' => ['target' => 'entity:' . (int) $e['id'], 'target_name' => (string) $e['name'], 'topic' => 'listing'],
3323:                 'directions' => !$private && $e['latitude'] !== null && !ListingActions::isMailbox((string) ($m['address'] ?? '')) ? 'https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=' . $e['latitude'] . ',' . $e['longitude'] : null,
3324:                 'url' => '/listing/view.php?id=' . (int) $e['id']];
3325:     }

3327:     /** The pin is only its ZIP area's middle (decisions/0058 §14): the map card says so and asks for the street point. */
3328:     private static function approx(PDO $pdo, int $id): bool
3329:     {
3330:         if (!class_exists('Geocoder')) require_once __DIR__ . '/Geocoder.php';
3331:         try { return Geocoder::isApprox($pdo, $id); } catch (Throwable $e) { return false; }
3332:     }
